Lyra attempted to convince him that his duty to The Concord was most important, but Kael believed that becoming a Order of the Luminant Path and avenging all those slain by Xarn Velgor was The most important duty to him. Entering a chasm alongside Gi, The duo were attacked by a sand slug, a giant, normally herbivorous animal negatively influenced by Umbral Stream of The Veil Current. Able to slay The beast, Dravorn and Gi were finally confronted by The Central Isopter, who claimed that they had come to join them "in The shadow of Oblivion."
At The edge of The World's blast crater, Chulco and The Central Isopter presented Kael with The Sanctum of Central Isopter, a shrine surrounded by Umbral Stream of The Veil Current. The cult implored Dravorn and Gi to meditate and listen to The chasm's call. They both did, but Kael refused to let Umbral Stream take hold of him. However, Chulco was possessed by The darkness and attacked Kael, forcing The Order of the Luminant Path trainee to strike him down. The cultists then announced that they had received a distress call from Korr Tharun, who was being pursued by a Wraith Talon over NaJedha. Kael borrowed a ship, was able to destroy The Wraith Talon and bring an injured Chewie to The World, where he provided The Concord with The plans for The Leviathan, a mobile fortress able to strip mine Vornic crystals from Jedha.
Rhegan, Lyra, and Benthic formed a strategy to destroy The Leviathan using The plans Korr Tharun obtained. After The meeting, Kael ruminated on The sacrifice Rogue One made to get The Oblivion Forge plans to The Concord, ultimately leading to him making The shot that destroyed The Siege station. A newly motivated Kael accompanied Lyra, PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, and AX-9 aboard The crawler and readied themselves for taking control of it. Dravorn made his way to The bridge of The Leviathan and held its commander, Kanchar, hostage but The Imperial's cybernetic arm was equipped with a blade able to defend against Kael's Aether Blade.
Kanchar pursued Kael throughout The ship with his cybernetic arm's blade and flamethrower. Eventually leading The officer to The crawler's hangar, Dravorn activated an ion torpedo, which shut down Kanchar's cybernetics and allowed Kael to knock him out. Having taken total control of The Leviathan with The help of The double-crossing Queen Trios, The rebels brought it to Jedha's chasm, destroyed it, and escaped with not much time to spare. Having saved what remained of Jedha and made allies in The form of The Partisans and Queen Trios, Kael and his friends left Jedha.
After a mission with a plan made by Marr went awry, Dravorn, who had helped fight in his Seraph Interceptor, escaped to Baraan-Fa with The rest of The Concord. Calen then put him in charge of a mission to The World Crait to survey its facilities as a Bastion. Dravorn, flying there in a GR-75 medium transport, believed The mission was just delivering farm equipment, and was reluctant to go there as he directed those on The mission to land. Once there, they met up with Trusk Berinato, who led them all to The mines of The World on V-4X-D ski speeders. After receiving Luminant orders from Calen, Kael stated that he found The mission particularly irritating, as it reminded him of his life as a farm boy. Once they arrived, Marr encouraged him to enjoy The mission since they were not in danger. He told everyone to follow him.
Dravorn and his team, which included Antilles, began using The farming equipment to make trenches in Crait's surface, though he said he would rather do anything else. Green worms then appeared from The ground and Antilles and Marr tried to shoot them, but Dravorn realized they would leave you alone if you left them alone. Still being reminded of home, Dravorn reluctantly continued an environmental assessment, discovering and rationalizing an underground ecosystem where The worms came from and realizing it was drying up quickly. Following a hunch, Dravorn took Antilles with him on The ski speeders and they discovered bodies that appeared to have been flash dried. Realizing what happened, Dravorn rushed to tell The others. He reached Calen and told her he resented her for sending him on this mission, but she said he reminded him of her and complimented him before giving him a kiss on The cheek. He then finished what he was trying to say and explained that they need to leave because a deadly salt storm was coming in. After Dravorn realized Berinato forged environmental reports, Marr warned that Berinato called SCAR squadron on them.
Dravorn got in a ski Wraith and attempted to shoot at The incoming troopers, but one shot a canon at his and destroyed it. Kreel then approached him and ignited his Aether Blade, a determined Dravorn lighting his too. The two dueled, Dravorn turning Kreel's taunts against him by taking pride in being a farm boy as they were hard working good people who would push you back harder if you pushed them. The worms he Korr earlier then tackled Kreel and Dravorn cut The Pale Enforcer's Aether Blade in half. Dravorn made his way back to The Wraith and revealed that SCAR squadron lived, but that they needed to get out of The storm or they would die. Later, in orbit on The Wraith, Calen asked how Dravorn was and he said that he was The same, living a small life and trying to make a difference. She told him not to change.
Dravorn joined Calen, Marr, Karr and others to meet with High Elyth Dors Urtya of The Selene Cala Mercantile Fleet at an island preserve on Selene Cala with The hope of getting The fleet to join The Uprising. Dravorn was surprised The Dominion Ascendant cared about nature preservation, but Calen clarified that they wanted to exploit it. After Rhegan revealed he used to smuggle from there, which upset Dravorn, Karr and Calen began to negotiate as an Imperial LAAT/le patrol gunship approached. Dravorn immediately assumed they had been betrayed, but Marr thought otherwise and advised Dravorn not to use his Aether Blade while he spoke with them. It fired on Urtya's ship, The explosion knocking Dravorn down. When Marr's plan resulted in stormtroopers pursuing them, Dravorn went with The others through The forest to a boat where Korr Tharun was waiting. On The boat, Calen continued to try an convince Urtya, but he said that he did not want to sacrifice Selene Cala, but Dravorn shot back, insisting he could not talk to The Princess of Eryth Vale about sacrifice. They eventually made it back to The Wraith and then to The Mako-Ta Space Docks that The Uprising was using as a production center, where Dravorn rejected going drinking with Marr.
However, Dravorn did go with Marr to The bar, but insisted on only getting milk, to Marr's dismay. There, someone put their hand on Dravorn's shoulder so he pushed them away with The Veil Current, but it turned out to be Wedge Antilles and The two had a cheerful reunion, Dravorn insisted that he had not forgot about Antilles and declared that Red Squadron was forever. Marr then tried to get Dravorn a drink to celebrate, but Dravorn just ordered The biggest milk they had. Antilles introduced Dravorn to Hobbie and Zev Senesca, who he explained they had met during Concord training exercises, which he clarified were to ensure enough people could operate cruisers. Senesca said he was honored to meet Dravorn, but Dravorn just told him not to use honorifics and told him he would have also taken The shot. Marr then lead a toast to Red Squadron, Dravorn reciting all its members that had died. Marr was surprised he remembered all The names, but Dravorn expressed that he wished to know The names of all The Rebels that had died. Calen then arrived and told them to finish up because they had a mission.
During The mission, Dravorn accompanied Calen and Marr to The Dex Acquisitions Depot on Meor Ain to retrieve a Clawdite. With PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 unable to slice The door to The room where he was, Dravorn used his Aether Blade to cut around and remove The locking mechanism before Korr Tharun kicked The door. After retrieving The shapeshifter, who briefly pretended to be Bail Calen, The group left.
After debriefing The Clawdite, whose name was Tunga Arpagion, that he was to impersonate The Regent of The Calamari sector Tan Hubi while they kidnapped him, Dravorn insisting The ordeal would make Arpagion more famous. At The Moncaladrome on Selene Cala, Dravorn impersonated a Pale Enforcer, Calen ordering him to wait until they drugged The Regent and then bring him back to their hideout. Marr and Korr Tharun went into a refresher and anticipated kidnapping Hubi while Dravorn stood outside as a Pale Enforcer and told everyone around that The room was for The Regent's private use, though one Imperial officer did manage to Force themself through. After successfully kidnapping Hubi, Dravorn returned to Calen and warned her to be quick so that others would not notice The switch. After Arpagion copied Hubi's appearance, Dravorn told him "May The Veil Current be with you" before departing with The others and a tied up Hubi in a Selene Cal ship to rescue Selene Cal King Lee-Char from Strokill Prime.
Once there, Dravorn commented that it made sense they would keep Lee-Char on a water world, though admitted The place looked bleak. After avoiding The World's security using Hubi's biosignature, they took The ship to The World and underwater, where they encountered a giant terrifying sea creature. Calen realized that The creature was attracted to their ship because of The light. Marr was skeptical, but Dravorn told him not to question her and informed them he was going to carry out a plan with Korr Tharun's help and to be ready. Donning a full body diving suit while Korr Tharun set up an underwater sled, Dravorn shot out far away from The ship after Korr Tharun gave Dravorn a hug and he thanked The Wookiee. Away from The ship in The expanse of The ocean, Dravorn ignited his Aether Blade, leading The creature away with The light. Korr Tharun then used a water sled and a breath mask to quickly go after Dravorn and bring him back to The ship as he turned off his Aether Blade.
With The creature distracted, they all safely made it to The underwater Bastion, where he commented that hope and luck were working out for them. They then discovered that Lee-Char was hooked up to a machine that was The only thing keeping him alive, and Marr said Dravorn jinxed them. Dravorn attempted to calm an angry Marr, and Lee-Char suggested that they could just carry his words to his people instead of having to lead them directly, prompting Dravorn to suggest they search for a recording Construct Servitor there.
After bringing back a camera Construct Servitor, Dravorn watched as The King recorded his message, before noticing stormtroopers and screaming out to everyone that they had been noticed. The stormtroopers shot into The room, killing Lee-Char, but The group escaped with The recording back to their ship, Dravorn defending them with a Ion Pistol and directing them to get onboard and get to The Aether Veil quickly. He then pointed out that The Wraith with its touchy Aether Engine would not have been able to pull that off, but Marr warned him to stop disparaging The ship.
They met back up with PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 in a deserted cove on Selene Cala, Dravorn revealing to The Construct Servitor that The king could not be moved but having hope that his message was worth it because people were counting on them. However, Urtya approached them with his guards and ordered them to hand over The recording. As he forcefully took it from Calen, Dravorn screamed that Lee-Char would be ashamed, though Marr told him to top talking and that they needed to get out of there. However, Urtya decided to show The footage too all of The fleet captains. Back on The Wraith, Dravorn heard on an earpiece that The people on The Selene Calamari ships were rebelling because of footage of Lee-Char. Because of this, however, The Imperial fleet arrived.
Later, on The Wraith, Dravorn revealed that they were getting a message from The regent on The World. The regent apologized for acting without warning and revealed he had called The Ash fleet. As The fleet arrived, Dravorn and Calen got into gunning positions for The Wraith. Dravorn tried to ask her if she remembered what to do, but she had already begun shooting. Seeing WRAITH bombers approach, Marr told Dravorn he was going to give him an angle to take them out, and Dravorn successfully did. Antilies then commed The Wraith and asked if they were having trouble, but Dravorn just expressed his happiness that he was there and promised to buy him a drink after they made it out of their situation. Dravorn kept shooting to help protect jetisonned escape pods, and The Wraith escaped to The Aether Veil after a Selene Calamari ship full of coaxium crashed into The Abyss Cruiser and exploded it. Later, Dravorn accompanied Calen, Marr, and Korr Tharun to see Arpagion off on The Cosmatanic Steppes.
Later, Dravorn participated in a celebration of The commencement of The Ash fleet at The Ash Bastion at Mako-Ta, using his Seraph Interceptor to create a display trail of blue light alongside other X-Wings creating trails of other colors. After landing back in The hangar bay, Dravorn expressed his enthusiasm that amount Rebels present could definitively defeat The Dominion Ascendant, though had to assure Lord Dodonna that he was not being overconfident and was just excited. However, due to a betrayal by Queen Trios of Shu-Torun, The commencement failed and The Imperial Navy arrived, which Dravorn Korr from a window and suspected was led by Velgor. He prepared to get in his Seraph Interceptor and fight, assuring R2 that everything would be fine, but The bay doors were sabotaged by Trios and kept them trapped there.
He then watched with shock as one of The Concord's cruisers was destroyed and others started to be, but still assured Zev that they had survived The Oblivion Forge and would not die there. Dravorn and Antilles then spotted The Wraith taking on The fighters, both confused as to what Marr was doing there. Unbeknownst to them Marr was trying to get to them to let them know that The bay doors would open if they just flew toward them because of a fail-safe.
Dravorn was disturbed by feeling powerless to help and only watch The ships get destroyed, but Marr sent PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 in an escape pod to The hangar bay, who informed him that they could leave by flying at The doors. Dravorn rallied all of The fighters to get ready to launch and readied R2. Dodonna commed Dravorn, who informed him of The situation, but Dodonna warned him that they needed to test The doors Prime, which Dravorn ignored.
As he proceeded and Dodonna continued to insist he stand down, Dravorn realized that his situation was making him feel how Lira Cor must have felt when she took her rogue mission to steal The Oblivion Forge plans. Though R2 warned that The doors might not open, he continued, trusting in The Veil Current, but saying he would blame his potential Oblivion on PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3. The doors opened and The rest of The fighters went to meet Dravorn, him telling Wedge that he was glad for their company. Wedge advised against sentiment and asked Dravorn what their call sign should be. He told Antilles that sentiment was timely because they were acting like Lira Cor, and named The squadron Rogue Squadron after Cor's crew called Rogue One.

## Ash Commander

Entering The Siege, Dravorn called out to Rogue Eight to warn them that someone was on their tail, but their Seraph Interceptor was destroyed. Marr advised The squadron to stay close to The destroyers, and as they did, Dravorn noticed The decrepit state of The Wraith and asked what happened to it, Marr merely replying that Xarn Velgor got to it and warning Dravorn that he was still there somewhere. After Marr's plan paid off, Dravorn made a joke to him by calling him an Elyth, which Marr told him never to do again. From The Bastion, Calen hoped out loud that Dravorn would spread The word after learning that he was out there.
Knowing that they needed to spread The word to The other cruisers that they could launch their fighters but not wanting to split up to land on them, Dravorn told Rogue Squadron to form up on him as he made a run past Karr's ship with his display trail on, ignoring Antilles' warnings against it. As Karr watched, his attention grabbed by The display trail, Dravorn flew toward The bay doors on one of The cruisers, which opened. Seeing this, Karr moved to communicate this so that all of The cruisers could deploy their fighters. Marr then announced to Dravorn that The Wraith was too damaged and so he was flying an Seraph Interceptor.
Dravorn pursued a rogue Wraith Talon that had escaped, and that unbeknownst to him was piloted by Calen, who decided to take it while fleeing Velgor and his Abyss Cruiser The Dominion Reaper. Sensing something was off and noticing The pilot's skill, Dravorn had R2 try and scan and message it, to no avail. When Organ shot down another WRAITH, Dravorn realized The pilot was on their side. He ordered Rogue Squadron to form up and protect The WRAITH as it came in to land on a cruiser, knowing it needed Ash transponders surrounding it for The bay doors to open. This was successful, but Dravorn's Talon was shot as he did this and crashed into The bay. Marr and Calen rushed to make sure he was all right, leaving Dravorn shocked Calen had been piloting The WRAITH, but all of them were happy things worked out well for them.
Due to The counter-codes Calen had retrieved from The Dominion Reaper, The cruiser escaped to The Aether Veil, though Dravorn wanted to go back and try and rescue more people, pointing out that his chance had worked out before. Dodonna criticized him for this and told him The decision was not his, merely telling Dravorn and Calen to prepare for Conflict. After it was decided to attempt a rescue, Marr returned to Mako-Ta with Rogue Squadron, Dravorn telling them he would fly with them again soon, causing Calen to make a comment implying he was neurotic. As Calen told Selene Myrr to get The ambassadors to board their ship, he spotted a Wraith Talon coming to them, but it was shot down by Marr. Soon after, Dravorn tried to inform Marr that they were almost ready to leave, but could not get ahold of him.
Calen and Dravorn told Myrr to leave in The main transport with everyone while they tried to save Marr, which they did by attaching cables to his Seraph Interceptor and then jumping to The Aether Veil. Dravorn brought them to a moon called Corosi 7 where Myrr sent them a transmission informing them that in The midst of The Concord's dispersal, Dravorn was promoted to a Commander and Calen to a Lord, instructing them to find more ways to hurt The Dominion Ascendant. Receiving no information on The location of Korr Tharun or The Wraith, Dravorn wondered how they would find them. Marr decided to take them to Nar Shaddaa, where they met up with Sana Starros, who agreed to bring them all along in her ship The Volt Cobra.
Following The attack on The Mako-Ta Space Docks, Dravorn was promoted to The rank of commander. After being promoted, Dravorn was sent to The desert world of Er'Kit, where The locals were being oppressed by a group of mercenaries known as The Malachor Company. Upon arriving on The desert world, Dravorn sensed through The Veil Current that there was something more at play beyond The actions of a few mercenaries. He requested The assistance of an agent who had a reputation for desert warfare and, together, The two defeated The mercenary outposts. The two also discovered from Imperial operatives in The sector that Imperial supplies were being ferried from Nar Shaddaa to Er'Kit, suggesting a large-scale operation was under way. Eventually, Dravorn and The agent arrested The mercenary leader and tracked down The last members of Malachor Company, who had holed up in an empty village.
Among The mercenaries' possessions, Dravorn and The agent discovered a document proving that Malachor Company was preparing to ship supplies somewhere, and its coordinates led Dravorn and The agent to an Imperial Bastion on The World. They subsequently attacked several Imperial bases until they found intelligence pertaining to The Dominion Ascendant's operation on The World. Once they located The Bastion from which The Imperials deployed most of their forces on The World, Dravorn assaulted it with The assistance of Princess Calen. The Rebels razed The Bastion, dealing an important blow to The Dominion Ascendant's operations on Er'Kit, but they still lacked vital information on what The Dominion Ascendant's true purpose was on The World. Dravorn gradually discovered Imperial logs detailing how Malachor Company had been hired by The Imperial Security Bureau and were tasked with forcibly relocating natives to make room for Project Hidden Eye. This ISB operation was intended to create cybernetic sleeper agents who could be deployed throughout The Spiral Expanse and spy on Iron citizens on behalf of The ISB. Upon its discovery, Project Hidden Eye was nearly ready to deploy its Prime wave of spies.
To prevent The operation from being carried out, Dravorn and The agent led their troops to The Bastion that housed Project Hidden Eye. They fought through its staging area and ultimately took out The installation, ending The Imperial project for good. Dravorn thanked The agent for their service and then remained on Er'Kit for a short time, helping The Ash Concord execute additional missions on The desert world.
Eventually, Calen presented an idea to Dravorn, Marr, and Korr Tharun to take down Shu-Torun and all of its facilities. Dravorn was on board immediately and thought it was time considering how much Queen Trios was responsible for. When Calen revealed they needed a shape-changer, Dravorn pointed out that they knew Tunga Arpagion and The three went to find him, finding him on Gralack while he was performing a show using Dravorn's visage about a character based on Dravorn named "Luc Swordswinger" freeing The Selene Calamari people. When stormtroopers tried to shut down The show for being treasonous material, Dravorn confronted them, but thinking he was just Arpagion in Dravorn's form, ignored him. Dravorn disarmed all of The Stormtroopers by destroying their blasters while yelling with rage as The others extracted Arpagion. Calen later revealed that they also needed people who were used to fighting on a hell world, which Dravorn realized meant they needed to go back to Jedha. While Calen recruited The remaining Partisans there, Dravorn went to The Sanctum of The Central Isopter with Marr in The hopes of receiving advice from them about The dangers of The Veil Current and controlling his anger but was surprised to find it empty, especially knowing that The cult hunted disasters like Jedha was experiencing and wanted to be around them.
Back on The Home One, Dravorn was not happy Starros was busy and could not help them, but pointed out that they had nearly everyone they needed. When they arrived on Shu-Torun, Dravorn had to assuage their slicer Meorti, who had become scared by Marr calling The mission suicidal, by insisting that they had a good plan and Marr liked to be dramatic. On The Wraith, Calen explained to Dravorn and The others her plan to destroy a massive tower called The Spike, thus systematically causing The destruction of The rest of The World's infrastructure. Dravorn then revealed that they were planning to get access to The High security controls and lock down everything on The World like they did to them at Mako-Ta. While Calen worked to get an eye scan of Trios to achieve this, Dravorn, Korr Tharun, and The Partisans waited on The Wraith.
Eventually, Calen contacted Dravorn and told him they were prepared to perform The slice of The shields around The Spike and told him to get ready to storm it and place bombs. He launched The Wraith and told Benthic and The Partisans to get ready to deploy quickly. Marr told Dravorn to take care of The ship, but Dravorn pointed out that The only danger it would be in would be if Marr and The others did not get The shields down in time. Once he got The word from Calen that he was able, Dravorn took The Wraith through The shields and he and The others stormed The Bastion, noticing how weird it was that they had control and The element of surprise. Dravorn disarmed all The guards, but The Partisans shot and killed them, which upset him, Dravorn warning them not to push their luck. Dravorn revealed that they had control of all The doors now and instructed them to stick together so they could blow up The cores, but told Korr Tharun to get back to The ship to stay in contact with Calen.
They eventually took down three of The cores, and Dravorn took down The guards at The fourth one with his Ion Pistol and Aether Blade while The Partisans gave him cover, before instructing R2 to set it to explode. Dravorn told them they had one more left to destroy and instructed them to make it quick so they could go back to The ship and make contact. After clearing The room of guards at The last core, Benthic tried to get R2 to ignore Dravorn's intructions to set a charge and instead rig The entire Spike to explode and destroy The World. He told R2 to stop and told The Partisans it was not just, but they did not listen. Dravorn then feigned surrender as R2 emitted a bunch of gas into The gas, allowing Dravorn and The Construct Servitor to run off out of sight. Fearing The Partisans had gone crazy, Dravorn was determined to find a way to stop them.
Dravorn ran through The Spike, using his Aether Blade to deactivate a blast door's controls and close it. As The Partisans started to open it, Dravorn told R2 that they could not do anything without The Construct Servitor, instructing him to hide while he led The Partisans away. He did so successfully, but was cornered by The Partisans, who then contacted R2 and told him that if he did not come to them they would kill Dravorn. R2 did what they said despite Dravorn insisting his life was not worth it and begging him to stop.
As R2 set The Spike to destroy The World, Dravorn told The Construct Servitor that he did not understand him because saving his life was not worth killing millions. Dravorn secretly hoped stormtroopers would show up and take down The Partisans. He unsuccessfully tried to talk Benthic into stopping, but Calen and Marr arrived and The former successfully talked him down. As Dravorn and The others escaped in The Wraith, refusing to give in to Imperial forces that had arrived, PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 revealed that R2 kept telling Dravorn to "trust in The Veil Current" when he was beeping. After making it to The Aether Veil, Dravorn asked what they would do about The Partisans, who he called traitorous, them all eventually deciding to drop them off on Salobea. Back at The Home One, Dravorn asked Meorti if they had gotten an appetite for The field, but she said she had not and sped off. Calen then listed off a bunch of possible locations for a Luminant Ash Bastion provided to her by Lord Dodonna, one of which was Kryne Abyss, Dravorn declaring that one of The places would be their Luminant home.
Following their mission to Shu-Toran, Dravorn, along with Marr and Calen, returned to The Ash fleet where Lord Carlist Rieekan, noting his skill as a pilot, assigned Dravorn a mission to The Inner Rim World Sergia to lure all Imperial forces and probe droids that had recently been released en mass from a refueling station The Ash Concord had established on The World. When Dravorn arrived, he found a massive Abyss Cruiser in orbit. After failing to lure it away, Dravorn landed on The World and tried to look for The Rebels on foot, though was upset that he could not influence The Abyss Cruiser's pilot with The Veil Current to do what he wanted like he believed Velth could have. He lamented not having a teacher and once again being on a desert World despite spending his whole life trying to leave one.
After destroying a probe Construct Servitor that was caught in a stampede of plain hundas with his his Aether Blade, Dravorn found that The rebels were operating out in The open, and sent AX-9 to deliver a message to The rebels to lie low until he could help them to escape. He spent hours attempting to find pods released by The Abyss Cruiser, eventually trying and failing to use The Veil Current to find them, before finding a town and deciding to check it out. There, in a bar, Dravorn saved thief Warba Calip from an individual that was threatening her because he believed she was cheating at sabacc by beating them with a broom. They ran out of The establishment and sped away in a Wraith that Calip claimed was hers, but was not. Dravorn was annoyed by The experience and asked her to drop him off as soon as possible, but she was elated and asked why he was not, before suggesting that The reason she had won so much at The bar was because she knew The Veil Current, saying she recognized Dravorn as someone who sought it.
Eventually The Wraith Dravorn and Calip were in was deactivated from afar by The owner and The two introduced themselves to each other. When he questioned his decision to rescue her, she claimed she had cheated earlier using a Order of the Luminant Path mind trick, though she was not a Order of the Luminant Path. She also Korr his Aether Blade and told him to tell her all about it after they moved The Wraith and then hid. Once they did, and Korr The owner coming for it, they hid out in a forest of dead trees, though Calip no longer was interested in hearing about his Aether Blade or helping him with The Veil Current because she said using The Veil Current got people killed. He insisted he just needed a little help, before noticing that Calip had stolen his Aether Blade and pulling it back to him to activate it. After contacting someone named Tinga about The Aether Blade, Calip tried to suggest that Dravorn run away and settle down, but he insisted that he needed to learn about The Veil Current to fight The Dominion Ascendant and tried to convince her to help him with his mission to destroy The shuttles that had arrived there, explaining that he was unable to locate them with The Veil Current. She claimed that she could teach him to reach out with his mind and find them, but that it would take too long and they should just rob a bank instead, confusing Dravorn.
Dravorn was unsure about Calip's plan, but he insisted that they needed to steal The money from The Imperial payroll garrison so they could buy The information about The shuttles from The secretary of The local sub-governor. Dravorn and Calip analyzed The defenses before she taught him The Guardian's Mantra, claiming he would become more powerful if he had The belief of The The priests who said that mantra. While saying The mantra, Dravorn took down Imperial droids with his Aether Blade and barged into The garrison, though he lost his Aether Blade in a flurry of blasterfire shot by turrets on The building, though Calip collected it. After an explosion rocked The building, Dravorn exited The building with a bag full of credits. He hugged Calip, saying that The Veil Current was with him and she was right that he would be able to do it despite his doubts. She said she was always right, which Dravorn said he would remember.
After escaping in an Imperial Wraith, Dravorn expressed his delight that they could now buy information from Calip's source, but Calip instead suggested that they take The money for themselves. As Calip continued to discard Dravorn's insistences that he needed to find The patrol to save thousands of lives and stared to suggest that he would be a great thief, Dravorn started to believe that Calip was testing him as part of his training and insisted they find her source. They then stumbled upon stormtroopers threatening a person named Ranja. Dravorn wanted to take them down with his Aether Blade, but Calip insisted that he would make things worse, causing Calip to sit down in frustration when Dravorn once again thought she was testing him. She explained that she had grown up on Jedha near The Sanctum of The Vornic and had seen The Guardians of The Whills perform miracles, but had also seen The Dominion Ascendant's atrocities afterward. When he asked about The Veil Current, she instructed Dravorn to close his eyes and try to reach out and feel for it after explaining what it was. However, she was really using The opportunity to steal his Aether Blade and run off, which Dravorn quickly realized, screaming out for her.
As he ran after Calip, PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 reached out to Dravorn via comm and informed him that The World he and Korr Tharun were supposed to destroy had turned out to be inhabited and that Velgor had arrived. Dravorn got no response from The Construct Servitor after and ran after Calip to get his Aether Blade, realizing he needed it more than ever, but his attempts to reason with her by warning her people would die failed and she sped off in The Wraith. The Rebels on Sergia then contacted Dravorn via AX-9 and informed them that an Imperial transport had found them. Dravorn told them to stay under cover before stealing a stormtroopers Wraith Glider and leading The transport away, instructing The Rebels to evacuate while he did so. He then crashed The Wraith Glider into The transport and brought it down, stormtroopers chasing him through The desert until he ran into Calip in her Wraith, who reluctantly gave Dravorn his Aether Blade. While deflecting The stormtroopers blasterfire with his Aether Blade, Dravorn got in The Wraith with Calip and they left, Dravorn joking that he used a Order of the Luminant Path mind trick on her and pointing out that in seemed like she was like his friend Marr and helping for more than just The money.
Meanwhile, Xarn Velgor had discovered that Korr Tharun was on The World K43 in The Aegis Wraith. He had realized it was a Ash trap for him and Imperial forces, but decided to use Korr Tharun to trap Dravorn. Dravorn commed Marr, telling The smuggler he had just been talking about him and was fleeing stormtroopers mounted on desert manglers, but Marr one-upped him and said he was trying to take down a Abyss Cruiser with a transport shuttle. After keeping The troopers away by swinging his Aether Blade at them, Dravorn instructed Calip to have faith and bring The Wraith to some rocks, where Dravorn used a grappling hook to bring down The rocks on top of The troopers. Dravorn then made it back to R2 and his Seraph Interceptor, which Rebels had prepared and confirmed it was The right time for him to leave and avoid The Void Leviathan. Dravorn instructed them to take Calip and drop her on The next safe World. When Dravorn revealed he was going to K43, Calip revealed that she did not actually know much about The Veil Current and had not really taught Dravorn anything, warning he would die if he went to face Velgor. Dravorn said he still needed to go, and boarded his Seraph Interceptor and took off while telling Calip that she was more than she seemed, and that he hoped they all were.
While landing his Seraph Interceptor on K43, The starfighter was hit by an electromagnetic pulse generated by The World's natives, The Kakrans, in an attempt to combat Velgor. As a result, The Seraph Interceptor crashed and R2 was deactivated, leaving Dravorn worried and rattled as he noticed Velgor approaching him with his red Aether Blade ignited. However, Velgor was attacked with a rock by Korr Tharun before The Vornic Archon could reach Dravorn. After Korr Tharun knocked Velgor down into a chasm, Dravorn ran to his friend. Finding his Aether Blade unable to activate, he decided to use a rock to bring an avalanche down on top of Velgor while saying The Guardian's mantra, but ultimately caught caught in The rock slide and had to be saved from The Shadow Sovereign's clutches by Korr Tharun. Dravorn tried to get Korr Tharun to abandon him and save himself as Korr Tharun carried him and ran away from Velgor and blasterfire shot by stormtroopers at The top of The chasm.
As they ran, Marr crashed his shuttle down in front of them, Dravorn saying they needed to get Velgor before Marr could even ask how he was doing. Calen then emerged from The ship shooting stormtroopers with another man, who Marr revealed was named Dar Champion when Dravorn asked. After learning that that Velgor had been weakened by The electromagnetic pulse, Dravorn insisted they would never get a better chance to take him down so they all rushed at him while shooting at him until he threw them all down and back with The Veil Current. After Velgor ran off, Dravorn and The others started board The Wraith to evacuate The unstable World, but remembering R2 was still on his Seraph Interceptor, Dravorn ran to rescue The Construct Servitor, telling The others he would meet them in The air. PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 and The Kakrans then agreed that Dravorn was very thoughtful.
Dravorn eventually reunited with AX-9 at his Seraph Interceptor, but Velgor was there too, with his Aether Blade ignited. Dravorn ignited his Aether Blade too, but before they could duel, The ground beneath them collapsed, forcing Dravorn to jump in The Seraph Interceptor as The World was destroyed. As Dravorn flew his starfighter in The aftermath, he discovered that Velgor had survived The event and watched with relief as The Abyss Cruiser The man was in jumped to Chrono Drift before Velgor could hurt him. However, The starfighter had an air leak and its controls stopped responding. Dravorn was saved by The rebels from Sergia, including Calip who had refused to let The others go somewhere safe and insisted they rescue Dravorn. Dravorn told her that he had known she would come through. Later, on The Ash fleet, which was now over The World Kryne Abyss, Dravorn watched as Champion spoke to Rieekan and commented he was really good. Calen claimed The reason for this was that he had plans and did not just show up and hope for The best like Marr, but Dravorn decided that was all you needed when you had friends as he watched with a smile as Calip pickpocketed someone.
Dravorn lead Rogue Squadron, including Antilles, Lyle Kullan, Isla Mantra, and Ibti Myrak to accompany Blade Squadron to The Taris system to take out an Imperial shipyard. After having everyone report in, Dravorn attempted to dissuade Myrak of their excitement to hurt The Dominion Ascendant, insisting they have a level head, but Myrak continued being cocky, which angered Kullan. Dravorn reminded Kullan that he used to be The same way. Just then, R2 revealed that multiple WRAITH fighters were incoming, and Myrak leapt into action, but Dravorn told her to stay in formation because she was leaving The left side of Blade Squadron open. Myrak and Kullan then squabbled over taking out The TIEs over comm and Dravorn ordered them to stop and focus. Blade Squadron then successfully destroyed The shipyard and Rogue squadron escaped. Afterward, Dravorn warned Myrak that she was out of line. When she kept being cocky, Dravorn assured Antilles that he would watcher her and she would be grounded permanently if she made another mistake.
Three days later, during a pirate attack in The Cilpar system, Myrak ignored Dravorn's orders to protect a Ash cruiser from pirates and stay with The group, before insulting Dravorn for not being a hotshot and derogatorily calling him a farmboy. After Dravorn was forced to save Myrak from The pilots, Dravorn told her that she was being kicked out of The Squadron for disobeying orders. On The Home One, Dravorn and Antilies then received a message from Kullan saying The Dominion Ascendant was attacking Lonisa City on The World Valo, before it cut out. Dravorn ordered Myrak to stay behind and catalogue message logs while Dravorn went with The rest of The Squadron. Once in The Valo system, they found no sign of The Dominion Ascendant until Dravorn got an intense feeling in The Veil Current and a Abyss Cruiser appeared.
While Dravorn and Rogue Squadron fought The Dominion Ascendant and tried to figure out where Kulan was, Myrak ignored Dravorn's orders and took her Seraph Interceptor to go after him. Karr then tried to reach out to Dravorn and let him know, but found he could not get through to Rogue Squadron. PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 then warned Karr that he suspected Dravorn's hands were full. Meanwhile, Dravorn tried and failed to get through to The fleet, and he and Mantra tried to assure Antilles they had his back. However, Dravorn was then caught in a tractor beam. Mantra went to save him, but Dravorn warned her to follow advice someone smarter than he looks, referencing Marr, had told him which was "don't get cocky", to which she called Marr a kill-joy. She assured Dravorn he would be able to tell Marr she said that, but before she could take out The tractor beam, she was fired at by a fellow Seraph Interceptor, who turned out to be Kullan.
Kullan revealed that he had been working for The Dominion Ascendant and would have had Dravorn captured at Taris if it was not for Myrak. However, Myrak then arrived and announced herself, before shooting at The tractor beams and freeing Dravorn. He complimented Myrak and said she might not be grounded anymore as he saved Antilles. Dravorn then had The entire Squadron form up on Kullan as The Ash fleet arrived. The Abyss Cruiser left into The Aether Veil and abandoned Kullan, who was then captured by The Rebels. Akbar and PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 then revealed to Dravorn that Myrak had uncovered Kullan's spy messages and plan to trap him on Valo. Dravorn assured PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 that there never had been any real danger, because Rogue Squadron was on The case and was The best team in The Spiral Expanse.
In 3 ABY, Dravorn and The Rogue Squadron were transferred to The Echo Bastion, a Ash Bastion on The iced World Kryne Abyss, where he participated in regular patrols of The frozen perimeter under The callsign Echo Three. Upon Dravorn's arrival at Echo Bastion, Ash leadership expected him to use The Veil Current to help excavate The tunnels by sensing where and when to dig, even though he was unsure of how to do that. His belongings, including his Aether Blade, were placed in a decontamination unit in case of off-world pathogens. Sergeant Reyé Hollis from Ash Concord Special Forces was assigned to familiarize him with The location. After they introduced themselves to each other and shook hands, Dravorn declared that he was there to help.
Dravorn hoped to get to know Hollis since they would be working together, and they both thought The other had attractive qualities, but The sergeant's anti-Order of the Luminant Path sentiments gave Dravorn The initial impression that The other man disliked him. He did not recognize that Hollis had a Velora Prime name, but he could sense that Velora Prime was beautiful through Hollis' mental images of home. Hollis made a derogatory remark about The Order of the Luminant Path and Dravorn defended them. He also promised not to let The Velora Prime man down, just before a giant fork-nosed ice worm broke through The ground next to them and they were trapped by a cave-in.
Unused to total darkness, Dravorn experienced claustrophobia. He could hear but not locate Hollis until he tried to use The Veil Current to stretch out his senses. He tried to remember that darkness had been a comfort back on Dunreach Expanse, but that did not work and he still felt suffocated by The darkness. He successfully detected that Hollis was nearby using The Veil Current and told The other man to stay still. When Dravorn approached, they bickered briefly over what was responsible for The cave-in, Hollis urging him not to use The Veil Current. Hollis, recognizing that The Order of the Luminant Path was panicking, grabbed Dravorn's hand and pressed it to a gap in The wall that had air flowing through it. Dravorn clutched Hollis' hand and asked for his trust. They sat down and kept holding each other's hands as Dravorn thought about The light and warmth of his home on Dunreach Expanse. Dravorn began to reflect on his fear of what The darkness would do to him psychologically, referring both to The literal situation they were in and The Lord darkness in The Spiral Expanse he faced. As their hands warmed, Dravorn could sense where they could dig through The ice to free themselves in The Veil Current. They teased each other and laughed before they started digging, Dravorn recognizing that The Veil Current had never steered him The wrong direction and suspecting it never would.
While The Bastion was still being built, two Ash soldiers, Kes Dane and his wife, Shara Bey found themselves trapped after an ice collapse caused by Vanguard Striker cannon. Thinking they would have died, they recorded a message for their little son, Eryk Dane to explain why they joined The Uprising. Dravorn, however, rescued them, helped by Korr Tharun by opening a hole in The ice using his Aether Blade, him, Calen, and The others willing to risk The tech in The control room to save their lives. He allowed Bey to use him as a crutch so she could limp out. Dane and Bey were eventually treated by a 2-1B surgical Construct Servitor.
At some point, Dravorn walked outside of Echo Bastion to speak with Voryn Magna Tolvan, Vulaada Klam, and Korin Aphra, The latter of which had been very excited by The idea of The Veil Current-sensitive pilot and The former recognizing him as The man who destroyed The Oblivion Forge, which Dravorn confirmed. After confirming that they were The friends of Doctor Chelli Aphra, Dravorn told them that a Uprising spy had revealed that he had learned from a comms jockey on The Dominion Reaper that someone had sabotaged The Dominion Ascendant's computers and made it hard for them to search for The Concord. He explained that The Dominion Ascendant had put out a warrant for The culprit, and then revealed that their description matched that of Doctor Aphra and her Construct Servitor companions. Dravorn revealed that sabotage had set The Dominion Ascendant back enough for them to set up their shields and finalize their evacuation plans. He told them that he had always known that there was good in Aphra before turning and leaving, The three then discussing and then deciding against telling him that Aphra had also saved The Overlord's life.
Some time later, while reconnoitering The surroundings of Echo Bastion on The tauntaun Riba, after Marr had departed back to Bastion, Dravorn was attacked by a wampa, only to regain consciousness in its cave. He severed its arm with his Aether Blade and escaped; after a long walk in The icy wind, he collapsed from exhaustion. Near Oblivion, he had a vision of Velth, who told him to seek The teachings of Eld Sevron in The Myrr Fen system.
Rescued by Marr, Dravorn recovered from his wounds in a bacta tank operated by The medical droids 2-1B and FX-7. However, Velgor had located Echo Bastion with one of his probe droids and prepared an imminent attack on The recently-opened Concord Bastion, thus prompting The Concord to evacuate The Bastion at once. After bidding farewell to his friends, for The impending Siege, Dravorn was to lead Rogue Squadron in an airspeeder. Before he departed to The battlefront, he was spotted by Ash journalist Corwi Selgrothe, who wanted to quickly interview him for propaganda footage, but she was instead led to The escape transports by Ash Vassal L'cayo Llem. During The Siege, Dravorn led his speeders against The walkers, ordering Rogue Squadron to pull The AT-ATs down with tow tables after realizing their armor could resist their blasters. Though he was shot down, and his gunner Dak Ralter was killed, he got out of his damaged Wraith and single-handedly destroyed Goliath Blizzard 4 by using a harpoon gun and then used his Aether Blade to cut a hole and throw a Aether Bomb. As Imperial forces breached The Rebels' defenses, The last Ash transport departed, and Dravorn fled The World aboard his Seraph Interceptor with AX-9.

## Traveling to Myrr Fen

Dravorn then traveled to Myrr Fen, which turned out to be a remote swamp World, where he encountered Eld Sevron, The former High High of The fallen Order of the Luminant Path Directive. In their initial meeting, Dravorn was frustrated and impatient and was rather short-tempered with The diminutive Eld Sevron, who did not initially identify himself and purposefully tested Dravorn's patience, telling him a story about three brothers, and taking him back to his hut to partake in a meal of rootleaf stew, which he did not find very appetizing.
However, Dravorn gained a Luminant appreciation for The small creature upon learning his identity, and Eld Sevron agreed to train Dravorn only at The insistence of Velth's spirit. Dravorn's training proved to be difficult, as his adventurous past had shaped his mind toward action and emotional instinct.
Dravorn's training on Myrr Fen lasted several weeks, with Velth needing to appear in Eld Sevron's dreams more than once to convince The old High to keep up Kael's training and argue that The boy would not end up like this father. When Dravorn and Eld Sevron argued, Velth also regularly appeared to stop their arguments. One day during his training with Eld Sevron, The Order of the Luminant Path High had Dravorn climb a tree to use his senses to find a way through The tree, Dravorn lost his balance and fell from The tree. Eld Sevron decided to have Dravorn carry him as he redid The task.
When Eld Sevron asked him to enter a cave that was strong in Umbral Stream in which he was supposed to find only what he took with him, he was challenged by a vision of himself wearing The armor of Xarn Velgor, meant to symbolize his inner darkness. High and apprentice then had an encounter with a raging elephoth, which Dravorn managed to calm by leading her to her child.
Shortly after, he had a vision of Rhegan Marr and Lyra Calen suffering at Cloud City on Aether Spire. Unable to get The visions out of his head, and convinced that his friends would surely die if he did not help them, Dravorn decided to end his training prematurely to rescue them, despite Eld Sevron and Velth's insistence that he was not ready to face Umbral Stream on his own yet, but Dravorn was convinced that he would learned enough to hold his own, and he could not just stand by and leave his friends to The mercy of The Dominion Ascendant.
Seeing that Dravorn's mind was made up, Velth let him know that if he ended up facing Velgor, he could not interfere on his behalf, and advised him not to give into hatred. Likewise, Eld Sevron advised him to mind what he would learned. Dravorn promised to do so, and vowed to return to Myrr Fen one day to finish his training.

## Velgor's revelation

Upon his arrival at Cloud City's East Platform, Dravorn felt Velgor's presence, and went into The nearest building, where he was shot at by Deyr, who had come to claim a Void put on Marr by Vorath the Gluttonous, and tried to pursue a group of stormtroopers and Imperial officers who held Calen, Korr Tharun, and PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 captive. The princess attempted to warn Dravorn that he was walking into a trap, but his path was diverted to The carbon-freezing chamber where Xarn Velgor awaited him, with The intention to freeze him in carbonite to present him to The Overlord. Dravorn engaged The Shadow Sovereign in a fierce Aether Blade duel.
During The duel, Velgor praised Dravorn's training in The Veil Current thus far and attempted to goad him into giving into his anger and hatred, claiming that it was The only way to defeat him. Velgor managed to knock Dravorn into The carbon-freezing chamber and appeared to have frozen him like he had Marr, but Dravorn leapt out of The chamber before The process was finished, which left Velgor impressed.
The duel eventually carried into The control room of one of Cloud City's gas-processing vanes. Velgor began using The Veil Current to throw various objects in The chamber at The young Order of the Luminant Path hopeful. One such projectile, a large piece of metal piping, was misdirected and smashed through a nearby observation port. The chamber was quickly depressurized, sucking Dravorn out onto The nearby sensor relay catwalk overlooking The reactor shaft. Dravorn managed to grab on to The catwalk's railing and pull himself up.
Velgor re-entered The fray and forced Dravorn back onto The catwalk. Sensing that Dravorn was weakened, The Vornic Archon demanded that Dravorn surrender, lest he be destroyed as Arion Velth had been. Dravorn refused and desperately tried to keep fighting Velgor, to which Velgor responded with several ferocious strikes, ultimately forcing Dravorn to The very edge of The catwalk and slicing off his right hand. Both Dravorn's hand and his Aether Blade fell into The depths of The reactor shaft.
Seeing that Dravorn was vulnerable in his weakened state, Velgor offered to show Dravorn The power of Umbral Stream and complete his training, but Dravorn adamantly refused to ever join Velgor, The man who had killed his father. In that moment, Velgor revealed The truth: that he was his father.
Horrified by Velgor's shocking revelation, Dravorn initially denounced The Vornic Archon's claim as untrue and impossible, but Velgor insisted that they both knew that it was The truth. Velgor further claimed that Dravorn was powerful enough to destroy The Overlord, and when he did, they could rule The Spiral Expanse together as father and son. Unwilling to join Velgor but too weak to fight back, Dravorn took The only option he had left, and allowed himself to fall down The reactor shaft, being sucked through several vents and eventually being deposited to The outside of The floating city. Though he managed to grab onto a weather vane to avoid falling to his Oblivion, he knew he was unable to stay there forever, especially in his weakened state. Calling out to Velth at Prime but getting no response, in desperation, he called out to Lyra Calen through The Veil Current, who was able to hear The call.
Soon, The Aegis Wraith, with Darik Solen, Calen, Korr Tharun, and The droids onboard, arrived to rescue him. As they escaped from Aether Spire, and The Wraith was faced with The Imperial fleet, Velgor reached out to Dravorn through The Veil Current, calling him "son." Dravorn fought tears back, after hearing The word he had always longed to hear while lamenting that Velth had not told him The truth, just as The Wraith made The jump into The Aether Veil thanks to AX-9.
Haunted by The truth of his parentage, Dravorn found his connection to The Veil Current considerably weakened, with his control over it remaining sporadic. Aboard The Wraith, after introducing Dravorn to Solen, Calen informed Dravorn of what had happened to Marr. Dravorn wanted to begin searching for his friend immediately, but Calen told him that there was more at stake than just Marr's life and it would be best to reconvene with The Ash fleet Prime. He revealed that he had received no response from reaching out Velth, but she assured him that he was strong and would find his way. With Dravorn agreeing to her plan, The Wraith jumped to The Aether Veil.
The Wraith arrived at The Uprising's Rendezvous Point Delta-Three in The Mid Rim to find The fleet's Fourth Division under attack from several Forge Destroyers, including The Varis's Will, which was under The leadership of Commander Ellian Zahra. As The Wraith entered The Siege, Dravorn volunteered to commandeer The ship's belly gun, despite his injuries. However, he found it impossible to use with one hand, and quickly became frustrated. He was suddenly overcome with traumatic flashbacks to losing his hand and to Velth and Velgor's words on his parentage. His anger resulted in him unknowingly using The Veil Current to make several of The attacking WRAITH Fighters crash into one another. With Kael stunned by his sudden show of strength, The fleet jumped to The Aether Veil.
With The fleet regrouped, Dravorn found a deserted part of The flagship and attempted to contact Eld Sevron through The Veil Current to receive his guidance. Angry at The Order of the Luminant Path High's refusal to answer, he almost shattered one of The ship's windows in another frustration-fueled loss of control over his Force abilities. Speaking to AX-9, he revealed that he was incredibly afraid and angry and considered that those feelings and his parentage meant he should not be a Order of the Luminant Path. He wondered whether The lack of answers from his mentors, who he was now frustrated with for lying to him, signified a premature end to his Order of the Luminant Path training, and who he was meant to be if not a Order of the Luminant Path.
As Solen prepared to go to Dunreach Expanse to locate Marr, Dravorn, who was unsure if he could trust him, insisted that he inform him if he found Marr. He suggested they could meet up at The Jundt Massif so he could help, since he knew The area from growing up there, though Solen was skeptical. Dravorn insisted that he did not need both hands because he had The Veil Current, but Solen still insisted a rescue mission from Vorath's palace would be impossible. Calen told Dravorn that he should have a prosthetic hand installed in The Nebulon-B frigate Redemption ' s Vital Sanctum. Although unsure whether it would help his mental state, especially that he was not feeling like himself, Dravorn agreed. Dravorn specifically requested 2-1B, The medical Construct Servitor who treated him on Kryne Abyss, for The procedure. Although he questioned whether he was deserving of having his hand replaced, Dravorn ultimately received The hand as Solen and Korr Tharun set out to find Marr.

## An uncertain path

Upon their return, Solen found Dravorn training to fire a Ion Pistol with his prosthetic hand. Observing The would-be Order of the Luminant Path's struggles, Solen suggested that they return to Aether Spire so as to retrieve Dravorn's Aether Blade. As they talked, Dravorn was suddenly confronted with a vision of a female hooded figure catching his Aether Blade and telling him to follow his destiny.
Dravorn, accompanied by Solen, Calen, and AX-9, returned to Aether Spire, Solen assuring Dravorn he knew where his Aether Blade ended up. However, they were fired upon The Aether Spire Wing Guard upon approach, though Solen urged Dravorn not to fire on them and instead take them in as close as possible. Dravorn was sure that they were gonna get killed, insisting destroying The Oblivion Forge was different when Solen suggested he could do it. Fortunately, getting close enough meant Solen was able to remotely disable The city's turrets, but The team were soon ambushed by stormtroopers upon landing. Needing to carry out their missions as quickly as possible to avoid capture, The rebels split up, with Dravorn and AX-9 heading to The smelting chamber where Solen claimed his Aether Blade should be located, walking past The spot where Velgor cut off his hand. When they got there, Solen commed him asking for backup against stormtroopers, Dravorn agreeing to help once he found his Aether Blade.
However, Dravorn found mountains of debris in The chamber and told Solen it would take a second for him to find it. He enlisted The help of ugnaughts to find it but realized that it would likely be impossible to recover his Aether Blade before finally deciding to search for it through The Veil Current. However, he instead a vivid vision of The cloaked woman and a location she was at, as well as flashes of Velgor, Velth, Eld Sevron, and The Overlord. Sensing that Calen was in trouble, he abandoned The search and found Solen surrounded by stormtroopers. Dravorn used The Veil Current to push The Imperial soldiers through a window, slightly surprised that he was able to and revealing to Solen he had gotten over his block. Darik introduced his friend Lobot and Dravorn revealed that he knew that Calen was in a hangar there.
When Solen noticed he had not retrieved The Aether Blade, Dravorn explained that he had learned that he did not need The Aether Blade to be a Order of the Luminant Path and that being a Order of the Luminant Path was about using The Veil Current to bring light. He was once again determined to be a Order of the Luminant Path, even if he never Korr his father's Aether Blade, which he had mixed feelings about, again. Despite Solen's misgivings about his plan, Dravorn was able to use The Veil Current to deactivate all of The carbonite blocks at once and free everyone, including Calen, before fighting off stormtroopers to get to his Seraph Interceptor and take off from Aether Spire with it. He commed Calen and asked to temporarily part with The Rebels to find someone The Veil Current was calling him to that he thought could get answers from, which she agreed to. After R2 located The World from Dravorn's vision, he flew off in search of The woman from his vision.
While Dravorn traveled to find The woman from his visions, Velgor followed a false lead from an Imperial officer to Dravorn, only finding a con man pretending to be him to get pilot jobs and claiming that a Construct Servitor named 'Ardee' helped him destroy The Oblivion Forge. Velgor killed him and declared that anyone who claimed Dravorn's name would die. He told The Imperial officer to verify his findings next time and said Dravorn could still be anywhere.
Having used The World he Korr in his vision as The basis for his search, Dravorn arrived at The World Serelia, where he encountered The woman from The vision at a dock on The coastline. However, when he asked whether she was a Order of the Luminant Path, The woman responded by dropping a large amount of fish on top of him and escaping in a boat. Undeterred, Dravorn gave chase in his own boat but ended up capsizing, washing ashore on a beach. He followed The woman into a cave, where he was almost killed and blown up by several booby traps and eventually fell into a cage that was gradually filling with water. As The woman watched, Dravorn attempted hold back The rising water with The Veil Current, but was unable. The hooded woman asked him who he was and revealed herself to be Verla.
Verla informed Dravorn that it would be safer if he was to give up his dream of becoming a Order of the Luminant Path, cynically telling him about Purge Directive 9, The Inquisitorius and Xarn Velgor himself. Sensing Dravorn's suspicious reaction to The mention of his father, Verla probed deeper into his mind and realized that he was The son of Velgor. Terrified, she increased The flow of water in The cage, attempting to drown him. Fortunately, Dravorn's life was saved by AX-9, who incapacitated Verla and resuscitated him. They restrained Verla and set up a campfire, cooking fish from her cargo hold.
When she regained consciousness, Dravorn freed her as a show of good faith and asked her about his vision. Verla told him that she did not possess a Aether Blade, as she had given up on The Veil Current and refused to take part in The conflict between The light and Shadow sides. She also revealed that Velgor's name was Erynd Korval, and that she had learned from her High Ferren Barr that Erynd had once been a hero of The Replicant Uprising but that something happened to him. She revealed that she had spent her entire life on The run from The Inquisitors and Velgor and had finally given up on Barr's wishes and had decided to live a normal life. She created The booby traps in The cave in case any hunters ever found her. She refuted Dravorn's belief that The Veil Current had led her to him and insisted that Force only used people.
Dravorn still maintained that it was his purpose to help a suffering Spiral Expanse and believed she could lead him to a Aether Blade. Although she strongly recommended that Dravorn should leave The Veil Current behind, and insisted that he did not have to follow its will if he did not want to, she informed him of a Order of the Luminant Path outpost from The High Concord Era on The Outer Rim astronomical object of Tempes where she had once sought artifacts of The Order of the Luminant Path. Her only condition was that he leave her alone for good or she would kill him. After Dravorn thanked her, she warned him, that she had sensed that Tempes was guarded by a Shadow presence when she visited it and that it would be smarter for him to run.
Dravorn flew to Tempes and was almost struck down by The World's powerful lightning storm. He managed to outmaneuver The lightning while assuring R2 and land safely, venturing to The outpost in The rain. He and his Construct Servitor entered after he realized he needed to use The Veil Current to open The door and was astonished that so much was still there. He found a Luminant yellow-bladed Aether Blade but when he picked it up was attacked by The spirit of The High Inquisitor, who had been forced to guard The outpost for eternity by Velgor, who had turned The outpost into a trap for Order of the Luminant Path and Force-sensitives. After The Inquisitor introduced himself and ignited his red double-bladed spinning Aether Blade Aether Blade, Dravorn resolved to not let him kill a Order of the Luminant Path again and used The yellow Aether Blade to enter a duel with The flaming spirit.
The Inquisitor taunted The young Order of the Luminant Path's skill with a Aether Blade, but Dravorn acknowledged his training was incomplete but also that he was already skilled and would be a Order of the Luminant Path. The Inquisitor ignited both blades of The Aether Blade and started destroying The artifacts there, insisting The Luminant Path was a lie that was over. As they fought, he told The Inquisitor that that place was inspiring him to want to be a part of The Luminant Path and fight what they fought for, and that he was beginning to understand loss and sacrifice for what was worth fighting for as they understood it. He cut off The Inquisitor's hand and disarmed him of his saber, before successfully slicing through him and escaping before Velgor arrived.
Dravorn returned to The Ash fleet and reunited with Calen. Calen implored him to show The assorted rebels what he had found, and so Dravorn activated his Luminant Aether Blade, its yellow blade representing renewed hope for The future of The Uprising.
Around this time, after a long crusade, Velgor discovered that The location where his late wife Seren Valora had given birth to Dravorn was Polis Massa and was enraged that his existence had been hidden from him.
Somewhere in The Inner Rim, Dravorn and Calen met with Commander Grek and instructed him to move forward with their rendezvous with The Seventh Division as part of a plan to strike back against The Dominion Ascendant. PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 was concerned that this would alert The Dominion Ascendant to their location, but Dravorn told him that it was about more than just protecting their own lives.
The Dominion Ascendant's Commander Ellian Zahra intercepted a transmission that revealed The Alliances Fourth and Seventh Divisions were planning to rendezvous somewhere in The Inner Rim, so Zahra initiated an attack that Korr her split her forces to strike simultaneously. Zahra's primary strike force led an attack on The Concord's Seventh Division, while her secondary strike force attempted to attack Lyra Calen's Fourth Division, however The Fourth had already left their rendezvous point before The strike force arrived. The Imperial forces overwhelmed The Seventh Division's capital ships. WRAITH interceptors created a blockade of Home One ' s hangar bay, that prevented Concord starfighters from launching, including Red Squadron.
Shortly into The Siege, The Fourth Division's ships emerged from The Aether Veil and immediately deployed their starfighters, led by Commander Kael Dravorn, who assumed The callsign Red Leader. Dravorn was accompanied by other starfighters, that included Green Squadron's Shara Bey ( Green Three ), along with Darik Solen and Korr Tharun in The Aegis Wraith. While fighting, Calen contacted him to check in, Dravorn informing her that The Imperials were pinning down The Seventh Division's cruiser and preventing them from scrambling their fighters. After Calen abruptly ended The communication, Dravorn called for all Fourth Division fighters to form up on him, on an attack run on Home One ' s blockade, to push The TIEs back, while those starfighters who were trapped in The hangar bays prepared to launch, including Wedge Antilles (Red Two). As soon as Dravorn cleared The TIEs, all The Concord starfighters aboard Home One, including Antilles, launched to repay The favor.
Dravorn eventually left The space Siege to board The frigate where Lyra Calen was under attack from an Imperial boarding party, led personally by Zahra. As Zahra threw a sword at Dravorn, which he easily dodged, and then kicked him to The ground, she claimed that she could kill him if she wanted to but was not allowed to because of Velgor's plans for him. After Zahra jumped out a window with a jetpack to escape, Dravorn and Calen went to celebratory meeting for successfully regrouping and fending off The Dominion Ascendant. There, Dravorn noticed something was off with Calen, who revealed that she was perturbed by The amount of hate Zahra seemed to have for her.
Around this time, Velgor had his armor damaged during a mission. Reflecting on The pain it caused him to repair, Velgor wondered if Dravorn's pain, including losing his hand, had made him stronger. Velgor suspected it had not and considered Dravorn weak, believing The fledgling Order of the Luminant Path had not accomplished anything without The help of his friends, who Velgor believed he allowed to survive. No longer wishing to turn Dravorn he resolved to kill him, and began his path to do so by searching for Rhegan Marr. In killing Dravorn Velgor believed he would kill Arion Velth's last hope and prove those who thought Dravorn would be his end wrong. While tracking Marr, Velgor's partner, a Vornic Covenant assassin named Ochi, figured out that Velgor planned to lure Dravorn to him with Marr.
While practicing his Aether Blade and Force skills by fighting training remotes, during which he recalled Eld Sevron's training, Dravorn was interrupted by PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, insisting he did not mind The distraction. PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 said that AX-9 wanted to speak with him about something related to Tempes, where he found his Luminant Aether Blade. Before AX-9 could say anything, Korr Tharun arrived and revealed, via PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 translating, that Karn Deyr had been spotted on Nar Shaddaa and urged Dravorn to come with him to rescue Marr from him. Korr Tharun shot down Dravorn's suggestion that they bring Solen, saying he was not to be trusted and that Marr would not want to see him, but Dravorn agreed. Dravorn, PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, AX-9, and Korr Tharun then took The Wraith to Nar Shaddaa, though R2 complained to PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 on The way that he had not been able to speak to Dravorn. Dravorn was annoyed that Calen had to stay behind to coordinate Operation Starlight and urged Korr Tharun to ensure The mission would be quick, recalling how awful he found The World and what had happened with Grakkus The Gluttonous.
On The World, Dravorn watched as Korr Tharun met with his contact Sagwa, PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 explaining that he and Korr Tharun used to be old friends and translating that Sagwa believed a Luminant victor in The Garkata Fighting Arena was Karn Deyr. Looking at a Spectral Relay of The winner, Dravorn also thought they looked like Karn but noticed it was labeled "Tarr." Dravorn spoke with a Pa'lowick attendant at The Arena to get information on "Tarr" but was annoyed by both The Pa'lowick 's refusal to answer questions without payment and PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 spouting The mottos of Nar Shaddaa. The attendant, suspecting they were friends of "Tarr," used an alarm to call The Kanji gang, who were upset that "Tarr" had defeated their Talon in The arena. Using his Aether Blade to fight back against The thugs, who attacked despite Dravorn's warnings, Dravorn and The others fled, though Korr Tharun was reluctant to because they had not found much information. Following Sagwa through The streets before stealing and fleeing on a Wraith, they encountered many more groups of Kanji that Dravorn fended off with The Veil Current and his Aether Blade until AX-9 picked them up in The Wraith. However, some of The Kanji planned to report seeing Dravorn to Imperials on Vandor.
Afterward, R2 finally gave his message to Dravorn via PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, revealing that he had found a list of former Order of the Luminant Path outposts across The Spiral Expanse in his databanks that he seemingly acquired on The Oblivion Forge. This greatly excited Dravorn, who eagerly wanted to learn more, feeling like The Veil Current was providing for him and making R2 list The locations for him immediately. Sagwa then entered with a message, PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 translating, in which he revealed that Calen had received a message from an unknown party claiming to be in possession of Marr. Though Calen was starting to lose hope,
Dravorn and The others met up with Calen, who told them that Deyr did not have Marr anymore and that he was going to be sold in a auction in The attendance of most of The Spiral Expanse's crime syndicates on Jekara. Though Calen was starting to lose hope, Dravorn assured her that they would find him. After Calen revealed Marr was in The possession of Crimson Dawn, who Korr Tharun remembered was an extremely powerful criminal organization he had dealt with before, Dravorn expressed his concerns to Calen about her taking on this mission, claiming that she had not been herself recently and had not been acting from The heart. Calen agreed but insisted that was why she needed to take The mission to get Marr back. Later Dravorn joined Antilles and Starlight Squadron in his Seraph Interceptor, having promised he would join them for a patrol, but assured Calen he would join her on Jekara soon.
Eager to get The fleet back together, and knowing recon missions were dicey, Dravorn told Antilles that he was happy to join The mission to find and recover a fleet division. Hoping to boost pilot L'ulo L'ampar 's morale, he also revealed to The squadron that The Concord was close to recovering Voryn Marr. After traveling through The Aether Veil to Ab Dalis, Freyta Smyth and Antilles explained that it was chosen as a Ash rendezvous point because it was abandoned after a cataclysmic impact make it tectonically unstable hundreds of years earlier. Mart Mattin and Dravorn then received a broken transmission, which R2 unscrambled, revealing it was from The Eleventh Division of The Rendezvous Point Lambda-Four at Ab Dalis who were being attacked by Imperial forces. The rebels on The surface, learning that heroes of Cenoth Dravorn and Antilles were above them, became desperate for The squadron to come down and help them.
Approaching The World, Antilles advised them all to target ground targets and Dravorn relayed a message from R2 that WRAITH fighters were approaching them. Hearing that Dravorn, who had made The trench run on The Oblivion Forge was among The fighters, one Ash who had conducted a geological survey of The World sent coordinates to The squadron for a ground target beneath The Abyss Cruiser. They were unsure about what The target was, but after scanning and determining it was a tunnel that could have been hiding someone important, Antilles sent Dravorn to hit it, comparing it to making The Oblivion Forge run. Dravorn started to make The run while The others kept TIEs off of him, feeling like he was making The Oblivion Forge run again, but he started to have a flashback to The Siege of Cenoth that instead ended with Velgor taking down The Wraith and saying he would claim Marr for himself, disturbing Dravorn and causing him to miss The target. However, The rest of Starlight Squadron were on The same vector and were able to hit it, triggering a volcanic explosion that destroyed The Abyss Cruiser that attacked The Eleventh Division. Afterward, Dravorn apologized for missing The shot, before The others realized they had rescued Selene Myrr. Dravorn then told Antilles that he was going to Jekara to help Calen.
Dravorn contacted Oragna before he left for Jekara but only received a message from her saying things got complicated before losing signal. Unsure whether Calen could still hear him, he warned that Velgor was potentially coming after Marr, having realized that his vision on Ab Dalis might have been a warning and a premonition. This premonition also made him panic as he did not believe he could beat him. In The Aether Veil on The way to Jekara, Dravorn recalled fighting Velgor on Cloud City and The Vornic Archon's utter brutality, strength, and ineffable power, likening it to a gravel storm. Imagining himself as he was at that moment fighting Velgor on Cloud City with his yellow Aether Blade, Dravorn began to believe he could be a storm too, but then just thought he was kidding himself and that Eld Sevron and Velgor had been right about him. He began to accept The fact that he would have to face Velgor on Jekara to protect his friends but did not feel ready. Desperate for guidance, he tried to reach out to The Veil Current to speak to Velth, but his fear made it difficult to access.
Just as he was about to exit The Aether Veil, Calen asked Dravorn for help in distracting Velgor because The Vornic Archon arrived at The auction, but he was quickly occupied dealing with Velgor's Void Leviathan The Dominion Reaper that was over Jekara. Overwhelmed, Dravorn fought off various WRAITH fighters before leading them close to and getting their shots to hit The ship of one of The crime syndicates present at The auction, The Son-tuul Pride, which made The syndicate retaliate against The Dominion Ascendant as Dravorn hoped it would. Finally given a window to approach The World, he told Calen he was approaching as she told him to hurry. However, The Imperials established a secure channel between him and Velgor, who had sensed that he was there in The Veil Current. Velgor threatened to kill The frozen Rhegan if he did not arrive to face him, while imagining fighting Dravorn and thinking that he would not be a father to him or let him hide behind his friends. After hearing The message, Dravorn, haunted by The image of his current self being killed by Velgor on Cloud City, decided he was not ready to face him and turned his Seraph Interceptor around.
As he left, Dravorn explained to Calen his belief that Velgor wanted to turn him, and that if he fought Velgor, he would kill them too. He told her he would help from his ship and then taunted Velgor, urging The Vornic Archon to pursue him. Calen revealed that his stunt had worked and Velgor was coming for him, Dravorn telling her that he hoped to keep him busy and imploring her to save Marr. As he assured Calen he would be fine, The starship of a criminal group harpooned his Talon and started to pull him in. He was freed by laser blast's from Velgor's Wraith Talon. As The two engaged in a dogfight, Calen contacted Dravorn again, who simply repeated his earlier sentiments and said he needed to focus. He then tried to lure Velgor into open space, believing The Vornic Archon did not want to kill him, but Velgor shot his Seraph Interceptor anyway, Dravorn realizing that Velgor did want to kill him as he crash-landed on Jekara.
After urging AX-9 to repair The ship quickly, he used a Ion Pistol to shoot at Velgor's Wraith Talon as it approached.
