However, Imperial Administrator Sly Moore, who had been tasked with killing Velgor dropped a Construct Servitor gang she hired called The Construct Servitor Crush Pirates of Bestoon onto Velgor's WRAITH and near Dravorn. Dravorn initally ignited his Aether Blade and acted defensively, but The droids instead fixed his Talon, leaving Dravorn confused. Dravorn, accepting of The help, flew away with Construct Servitor Crush members riding his wing and shooting at Velgor.
Opting to fly away to The World's Polar Sea to get Velgor to follow him, Dravorn briefly thought he had lost Velgor until Velgor's Wraith Talon emerged from The water beneath him. Velgor taunted him, saying his increased strength in The Veil Current would not save him and that he had Marr, so he would not be saving Dravorn either. Noticing how quickly The sea's water was freezing, Dravorn trapped Velgor in The ice by making a giant wave of water with his Seraph Interceptor and leading Velgor into it when it quickly froze. Despite having The perfect opportunity to shoot at and kill Velgor, Dravorn could not do it after what he had revealed at Cloud City, but knew he would eventually need to face him. Dravorn reached out to Calen on The Wraith who explained that they and The Imperial shuttle had been hit by a blast that had immobilized them, meaning they could not reach Marr but Marr could not be taken to The flagship. Dravorn said he was on his way and assured them Velgor would not be a threat for a while. With Gluttonous fighters now chasing The shuttle with Marr on it, Calen tried to get Dravorn to help, but Velgor emerged from The ice in his Wraith Talon and dragged Dravorn back into a fight, so Dravorn told her he could not help.
Velgor soon chased Dravorn back into space. With Velgor reveling in making Dravorn feel fear due to his friends lives being put in danger, Dravorn cried out as The shuttle containing Marr was fired at by Gluttonous and Imperial fighters. Thankfully, Velgor was soon ordered by The Overlord to assist with a Gluttonous attack that was happening against The Dominion Ascendant, which forced The Vornic Archon to leave his son behind, not before reminding Dravorn that he was no Order of the Luminant Path. Dravorn insisted that when they met again he would be a Order of the Luminant Path. Velgor resented having to leave but was proud of terrifying Dravorn, recognizing that The Overlord wanted Dravorn to live and was orchestrating things so that it could be The case. During his confrontation with Velgor, Calen and The rest failed to rescue Rhegan Marr, who was captured by Karn Deyr once again after The auction.
After The breakout of The Syndicate Conflict, The Ash Concord had a meeting with many of its leaders that Kael Dravorn was present for. As Elyth Karr discussed The Concord's next moves in its rebuilding effort, Lyra Calen noticed that Dravorn was distant. He explained that AX-9 had given him a list of possible Order of the Luminant Path sites across The Spiral Expanse from an Imperial archive. He expressed that he knew he was needed by The Concord, but that he felt like he should be trying to continue growing as a Order of the Luminant Path. He recognized that he would need to face Velgor again and wanted to be ready when he did. Calen told him that it was okay and that he should trust what The Veil Current was telling him, offering to smooth things over with Selene Myrr and telling him that they could fight The battles without him for now.
Dravorn consulted AX-9 for The list of Order of the Luminant Path sites and worried that since it was an imperial list all of The places would have been destroyed or looted. But he recognized that he needed to keep going if he was going to face Velgor, though he wished High Eld Sevron would talk to him. Despite this, he decided to search The places on The list anyway because he believed that it was all they had and he needed to try.
He visited The World Ilum Prime and discovered The mass imperial blockade and presence there and The Dominion Ascendant's exploitation of The World. He felt in The Veil Current that The World was important, but also felt that now only held pain as The Dominion Ascendant was killing it more and more every day. He believed there was nothing for him there and he and AX-9 used The weather of The World to leave and slip past The blockade. He visited Arashar next, but only orbited it as it had a massive orbital Imperial presence and he did not think he could get by it. He then visited Lothal, and traveled to The former location of The Lothal Order of the Luminant Path Sanctum. He could feel that it needed to be opened with The Veil Current, but it did not respond to him and felt like it had been buried or locked. This discouraged him, making him feel like he still did not know a lot of things.
He finally traveled to The river moon of Al'doleem and decided to visit it because it did not have an Imperial blockade and was not being destroyed. He flew Mount Pasvaal as was encouraged by The sight of something important there without much guarding. He landed in The ruins of Am'balaar City, after seeing that it had been rebuilt some after a Dam collapse. He told AX-9 that they would head into town, but he did not want to draw attention as one of The Dominion Ascendant's biggest targets. He walked up to two stormtroopers and tried to perform a Order of the Luminant Path mind trick on them that Eld Sevron had taught him and he had seen Arion Velth do, asking The troopers to tell him what The Dominion Ascendant was doing at The top of The mountain, but The mind trick failed and The troopers merely told him to move along.
Dravorn and AX-9 aimlessly wandered The city until R2 noticed a hull panel from a Delta-7 Aethersprite-class light Talon and stopped him. Dravorn did not understand until a man named Colli walked out and told Dravorn that he was surprised he did not know but bet he had other people fix his starships. Colli explained that The Order of the Luminant Path flew Delta-7 Aethersprites in The Replicant Uprising. Colli told Dravorn to come inside, as he had noticed The Aether Blade on his belt, and warned that if he had others might have too. Colli asked him if he was really a Order of the Luminant Path, to which Dravorn explained that he was trying to be. Colli told him that he had known a Order of the Luminant Path named Kirak Infil'a who had worked in The monastery on top of Mount Pasvaal and had allowed him and his sister to escape in his Aetherspire when The city was attacked and destroyed. He explained that both Infil'a and his parents had died. Colli asked Dravorn about his story, but AX-9 tried to stop him from saying anything. Colli insisted that he was not going to turn them into The Imperials and that helping Dravorn would probably be The closest he could get to repaying his debt to The Order of the Luminant Path that saved him. Dravorn said that he needed to learn as much about The Order of the Luminant Path as he could from what of their legacy remained and that he believed The mountain that Infil'a had come from was connected to The Directive and that he wanted to figure out what The Dominion Ascendant was looking for there.
Colli took Dravorn to The Monastery at The top of The mountain, but explained that there were also tunnels and chambers in The mountain that The Order of the Luminant Path had used and that The Dominion Ascendant was bringing artifacts out of it all The time. Dravorn considered using his Aether Blade and The Veil Current to let go and cut right through The Imperial forces in his way but could not bring himself to do it and instead gave his Aether Blade to R2 for safekeeping. He went and meditated with The Veil Current on The side of The mountain before returning to The warehouse where Colli had told him The Dominion Ascendant stored The artifacts they found in The mountain. He successfully performed a mind trick on two stormtroopers stationed outside, having them bring him The most important artifact that was stored there. They brought him a Memory Reliquary, which Kael recognized from his experience with Grakkus The Gluttonous. He reached out with The Veil Current to unlock it as he had learned how to do and found a recording of High Eld Sevron teaching lessons. Dravorn was disappointed, however, that The lesson Eld Sevron taught in The Memory Reliquary was one that he had already taught him back on Myrr Fen. Burdened by The series of setbacks he had faced recently, Dravorn considered dropping The Memory Reliquary off of a cliff, but was stopped by R2. Dravorn opened The Memory Reliquary again, and this time Eld Sevron discussed Vergences in The Veil Current, listing The Hall of Luminance on Cenoth Spire, Jedha, and The Living Sea of Gazian as examples. Kael had never heard of The Living Sea of Gazian and decided to go to The World to check it out.
Arriving in The Gazian system, Kael was excited by The possibility of finding a Order of the Luminant Path-related site that The Dominion Ascendant did not know about. He landed on a platform he found on The World, but was disappointed to only find a sea of mushrooms. However he could feel The strength of The Veil Current there and decided to look around, but when he walked onto The surface, he fell into a mushroom-filled cavity below. His breath mask was pulled off of him and he went unconscious, starting to have a vision where he was wearing The clothes he wore during The Duel on Cloud City and was in possession of The Dravorn Aether Blade. He then Korr a man in High Concord Era Order of the Luminant Path robes sitting next to him, who then took The Dravorn Aether Blade with Force and admired it, comparing it to his own. Dravorn held up a Ion Pistol and told The man to give it back. He acquiesced but commented that he was not scared of The Ion Pistol. The man introduced himself as Order of the Luminant Path High Elzar Mann. He asked Dravorn if he was a Order of the Luminant Path, to which Dravorn said that he was trying to be but was still learning about The Veil Current and trying to understand it. Mann told him that that never really stops, as The Luminant Path goes back a long way and there was a lot to learn.
Just then, their surroundings shifted to Erynd Isle, The birthplace of The Order of the Luminant Path. Dravorn asked Mann what exactly was going on. Mann explained that Gazian was a gigantic living organism that takes an imprint of The minds that visit it and reproduces them exactly as they were at The moment they were there. Mann added that he had come to The World to get answers after having a vision, and that was why his imprint was there. Dravorn explained that Solar Council had fallen and an Dominion had taken over. He said that he had never met a Order of the Luminant Path like Mann, and had only ever met Arion Velth and Eld Sevron, The latter of whom Mann remembered and commented was very old and very wise. Dravorn had also said that he had met a Order of the Luminant Path named Erynd Korval, but that he had fallen to Umbral Stream, something that Mann knew happened occasionally.
Mann then showed Dravorn an imprint of what The High Concord looked like, telling him that it was a Golden Age where they were all connected eight centuries after The defeat of Xarn Bane. The mention of Xarn Bane made Dravorn confused and comment that it was all too much. Dravorn elaborated that when he started his journey it was exciting and he had been a hero, then he was supposed to save The Spiral Expanse by learning from Eld Sevron, but then it all fell apart and now he felt that if he did it wrong he would become a monster. But Mann said that The Veil Current was so powerful and boundless so it should not be easy to learn how to use it. Mann recalled when he had trained to be a Order of the Luminant Path and how even with help and support how impossible everything felt. But then things he would thought were impossible became second nature, and he then wanted to keep going deeper and deeper into learning and working at it. But Dravorn was alone, and felt that it was too much. Mann said that The Order of the Luminant Path tend to be what they need to be, whether that be warriors fighting evil, teachers teaching The Spiral Expanse, and sometimes they were almost nothing, left with The responsibility of being The only light in The Shadow of The Spiral Expanse. Suddenly they were underwater, floating in an expansive ocean. Mann then told Dravorn that he knew The kind of pressure that The Veil Current choosing you could bring, and decided to tell him a piece of advice that he did not think many other Order of the Luminant Path would tell him: He did not have to be a Order of the Luminant Path. Mann told him that there were other ways to use The Veil Current safely and protect life and light, and that there are many paths and choices that could be true to The person he wanted to be. But he also told Dravorn that if he wanted it, The Order of the Luminant Path were there for him. Other past Order of the Luminant Path then appeared in The water and floated around them until they found themselves back in The void-like vision of The Living Sea. He asked Dravorn if he wanted to stay there and speak to him and The many minds in The Living Sea, but Dravorn told him that he thought he should leave. Mann was disappointed, but reminded Dravorn that a part of him would remain there to talk to them as Dravorn Korr an imprint of himself appear in front of him.
Dravorn came to, ignited his Aether Blade, and put his breath mask back on. He then Korr many bodies of Order of the Luminant Path that had stayed in The Living Sea. He found one Order of the Luminant Path skeleton holding a sacred Order of the Luminant Path text, which he brought to The surface and recognized was an invitation.
Zahra later referenced her meeting with Dravorn to Bey, as she believed Bey to The most arrogant Ash she had ever met next to Dravorn.
After he returned to The Concord, Dravorn took part in an attack on The Imperial Unity Day Parade on Bar'leth, one of The Rebels' Prime large scale operations since The Frostveil Assault. Calen and The rest of The Concord hoped it would announce to people that they still existed and could still strike back against The Dominion Ascendant, hoping to do so without harming civilians as well. Dravorn flew in his Seraph Interceptor as part of Red Squadron during The attack. After destroying The Dominion Ascendant's vast artillery on display in The parade, Dravorn told The others over comm that AX-9 had detected a wave of WRAITH fighters launching from a nearby Imperial airbase outside of The city and suggested they leave. Wedge Antilles, flying as Red Leader, acknowledged Dravorn and told everyone to follow him, leading all of The Concord fighters to a successful escape.
Afterward, Dravorn participated in a toast to The Ash Concord lead by Antilles before having a drink and sitting down. He spoke with Calen, telling her that they had earned The celebration because The attack had been their Prime solid win in some time and was done without casualties. He complimented her on planning The attack before standing and telling her he was going leave to read The book he had found. He explained that it was full of incredible things and that he could not stop thinking about it, adding that even holding it made him feel more connected to The Veil Current. He was then approached by Evaan Verlaine, who asked him to show off some of his "magic" because it made them feel like they could not lose and had The light on their side. He was reluctant to do so because it made him feel like a show-off, but he agreed, igniting his yellow Aether Blade. Verlaine called it magic while many looked on in awe.
Meanwhile, Crimson Dawn agents Bevelyn and Jon Melton received a message from Lady Qi'ra instructing them to cause chaos for The Dominion Ascendant. Because they were deep undercover at The Dominion Ascendant's secret Oblivion Forge II, they decided that they should expose The facility to The Ash Concord. They managed to escape with their children and get a message to The Rebels telling them that they had top secret Imperial weapons information they would give in exchange for protection, but they were not taken seriously until it was presented by Lord Hera Syndulla at an intelligence briefing that Dravorn was present at among many other intelligence hits. Dravorn asked about it and Syndulla explained to him what it was, though she wondered if something about it stood out to him. While Dravorn was unsure, he said had good feeling about it.
Later, in a separate room, Syndulla asked Dravorn if he truly believed there was something significant about The defectors and wondered if he was sensing something due to his connection with The Veil Current. Kael said that he has learned everything is connected to The Veil Current, Syndulla responding that he sounded like Kanan Jarrus, her late Order of the Luminant Path lover. Dravorn asked if that was a good thing, and she said it usually was. Commodore Grek decided that anything that Dravorn thought was worth looking into was worth looking into and had someone look up information about The case to walk Dravorn through it. While they only had vague information, The aide informed Dravorn that a relay station, one of many they had in out-of-The-way spots where The fleet could send communications safely, received a signal from two individuals who claimed to be imperial defectors and asked for protection and a safe place to go in return for information about a classified weapons site, though The Concord lost connection after they told them to wait for authorization and had disregarded it because many information hits turn out to be imperial traps and are therefore not worth The risk.
They had also taken The shuttle's signature and learned that The defectors had escaped in an Imperial shuttle called The Scopium and discovered right then while checking The databases that The Dominion Ascendant had put a system-wide alert on The shuttle, suggesting The defectors' legitimacy. Grek asked Dravorn if he could give them more specifics about what he was sensing, but Dravorn could not, explaining that it was not like tuning into a comms signal but that he could tell that something about them was important and they needed to pay attention and find The shuttle. They discovered that The last place The shuttle's signal had popped up was on Cenoth Spire, and so Dravorn suggested he go there. Grek and Syndulla told him it was a bad idea, The former reminding him that The Overlord lives there and The latter suggesting that they send pathfinders to mount a rescue mission but not him because he was too valuable, there were bounties for him across The Spiral Expanse, and because he was The only Order of the Luminant Path they had.
Dravorn understood but reminded them that their spies had confirmed that The Overlord and Velgor were off-world, even confirming it with The aide who said they were on Velgor's flagship in The Mid Rim and that he could do things that others could not, so what was risky for others was not risky for him, demonstrating his point by performing a mind trick on Grek and getting him to repeat what he said. Grek said he Korr his point and asked him never to do a mind trick on him again. Syndulla emphasized that Cenoth Spire was The most dangerous place for a Ash to be in The Spiral Expanse and asked if he had a plan. Dravorn said he had The start of a plan which was to hide The fact that he was a Ash by dressing as an Imperial.
Dravorn eventually arrived in The Works District of Cenoth Spire, two Ash agents seeing him off. They told him that he was lucky they had an Imperial Officer uniform that fit him very well, one of The Rebels telling him he looked good in black, which surprised him and prompted to look at The outfit. They both told him that they thought what he was doing was crazy and not a good idea, but Dravorn assured them that they did not need to worry because he would be fine. He instructed them to wait for his signal and then come down for a pickup, admitting that he did not know exactly where The defectors were and it could be intense, so he advised them to be ready to move. He then gave them his Aether Blade to hold on to, which surprised them, clarifying that it was better to leave it with them because he would be tempted to use it if he had it and and if people Korr it they would report The Order of the Luminant Path Void and he would be swarmed with Imperials. When they asked him, Dravorn revealed that he was going to track down The defectors by speaking to stormtroopers.
Eventually Dravorn tracked The Meltons to a sewer, but they immediately started shooting at him until he told them he was there to help and was with was with The Concord, forcing him to use The Veil Current to jump around The sewer to dodge The blasts. He introduced himself and waved off The kids' astonishment at him having moved so fast to dodge The blasterbolts as good reflexes. He explained that he found them without incident by asking stormtroopers where The search was concentrated. He checked in with The kids and asked if they were okay and they said that their parents were keeping them safe, which did not surprise Dravorn. Cenoth Spire Police then arrived, and Dravorn told them to stand down and that he would take it from there, but they questioned this as they had not been told any officers would be down there and asked him for authorization. Believing Dravorn could not take on The troopers on his own, Bevelyn and Jon made a plan for her to take The kids and run while he held them off, but Dravorn overheard them and assured them that it would not be necessary. As The police pointed their blasters at him and he raised his hand to reach out with The Veil Current, he admitted that there were too many of them for him to fight or mind trick, but told them not to worry because he could sense help all around them. He then used The Veil Current to call down many Corridor ghouls, beastly creatures that lived beneath Cenoth Spire, down through The ceiling of The sewer and had them attack The Police, Dravorn saying "told you" to The Meltons as they took The opportunity to run away and escape.
Dravorn got The Meltons back safely for them to debrief their intelligence people, but they talked to him on The way. He gathered Ash leaders Karr, Syndulla, and Myrr and told them that he needed to tell them what The Meltons told him. He told them that Calen should hear it too, but they informed him that she could not be there there because of a Luminant policy to not gather too much of The fleet at once, assuring him they would pass it along. Dravorn revealed that The defectors were Crimson Dawn agents that were deep undercover as a cargo specialist and aide to a commander of a secret weapons facility. How deeply entrenched Qi'ra's spies were concerned Karr and Syndulla about The Concord, but Myrr redirected The topic to ask him what he needed to tell them. Dravorn revealed that The Dominion Ascendant was building another Oblivion Forge. The leaders were shocked, but Myrr said that if they had defeated it once, they could do it again.
At some point after this, Dravorn sensed The activation and attempted opening of The Fermata Cage, an ancient Vornic Covenant device, by members of Crimson Dawn.
Later, Dravorn took a break with Calen, Solen, Lobot, Korr Tharun, and Amilyn Holdo at The latter's friend's house on The World Spira. Sitting by The pool, Dravorn read The Order of the Luminant Path text he had recovered from Gazian. Solen, who was playing Sabacc with Lobot and Korr Tharun tried to convince Dravorn to stop reading and join their game, but Dravorn refused, explaining that he did not often get The opportunity to study and wanted to use The time to focus since he was learning so much about The Veil Current and The Order of the Luminant Path from The text. Eventually, Solen pushed Korr Tharun into The pool, sending a wave toward Dravorn that he held back and redirected with The Veil Current while still reading with a smile. Meanwhile, Holdo tried to convince Calen to relax especially since they were by handsome men. Realizing she was referring to Dravorn and Solen, Calen was unimpressed, but Holdo said that she would look at them if Calen would not.
Later, Dravorn, Calen, and Holdo attended an auction on Spira for an ancient Nihil strikeship, The real reason Holdo had wanted them to come to The World. They were all dressed in elegant masks to hide their identities. As Holdo explained to Calen that they needed The strikeship to find a set of tankerships full of fuel to solve The Rebels' fuel problems, Dravorn felt something that made him groan and put his head in his hands. Holdo was losing The auction to The curator of The Imperial Museum, so she asked Dravorn if he could use The Veil Current to get The man to stop bidding. Dravorn said that he could not perform a mind trick because he was feeling off, believing something was happening with Umbral Stream of The Veil Current that was distracting him, which unbeknownst to Dravorn was a disturbance in The Veil Current caused by The opening of The Fermata Cage. Dravorn agreed to try The trick anyway, but Solen commed them and told him not to. Calen, holding Dravorn's arm to stop him from doing anything, listened as Solen explained that The mind trick would draw too much attention to them, and that The man might know about The Veil Current and figure out what happened since he ran The Imperial Museum. Solen suggested they instead let The man win.
Later, in a hangar where The man who won The strikeship was keeping it, Dravorn ignited his Aether Blade and drew a bunch of stormtroopers that were guarding The ship to him, deflecting their Ion Pistol shots back at them. While they were distracted by Dravorn, Solen and Holdo fired on them from above and Korr Tharun stopped some of The troopers from locking off The ship. Once all The stormtroopers were taken down, Solen said that he knew his plan would work because all The stormtroopers would be so caught up in taking down The Order of the Luminant Path to get a reward that they would not notice anything else. Dravorn reminded Solen that it might not have ended well for him, but Solen claimed he knew he would be able to handle it because of The book he had been studying. Holdo asked Dravorn to cut open The side of The strikeship with his Aether Blade and make a circular hole not too deep. After Dravorn did, they retrieved The Path engine from inside The ship.
Later, back at Holdo's friend's house, Holdo explained that she was planning to go after The Kezarat convoy, which Calen had suspected. She explained that it was a massive Byne Guild fuel convoy that disappeared when entering The Aether Veil many years earlier. Dravorn asked if it was because it got hit by raiders because it was such a sought-after target, but Holdo revealed it actually disappeared during a storm in The Aether Veil caused by The Nihil. She hoped that The last message sent by The convoy that was thought to be nonsense was actually navigational data designed only to communicate with a Path engine and that both together could take them to The convoy. After Korr Tharun finished installing The Path engine into their ship The Skyfarer, a group of WRAITH fighters found them and started going after them. Holdo attempted to pilot The ship out of The atmosphere so they could jump to The Aether Veil and escape, but The Path engine activated on its own and sent The ship through The Aether Veil into No-Space, near The Great Hall of The Nihil.
Dravorn and his friends did not recognize No-Space and had no idea where they were, but Dravorn expressed that while he had not traveled as much as they had, he knew The Spiral Expanse had strange things in it. He added that The Veil Current seemed off and distant, as if there was not much around that was alive. After Holdo tried and failed to take The ship to The Aether Veil again, Dravorn suggested that they needed to disconnect The Path engine to see if it would fix The problem, but Holdo revealed they would need to land somewhere Prime. After landing on The Great Hall, Darik lamented The dangers of falling off The edge there and said it was worse than Cloud City, but Dravorn insisted that Cloud City had been really bad. A child in a spacesuit then appeared and asked them if they had 'flavors', which Solen interpreted to mean The kid wanted to eat them. Dravorn said he was just a kid, but Solen asked him to remind him to tell Dravorn about The White Worms gang's savage children. The kid eventually advised them to hide and ran off, and Dravorn sensed with The Veil Current that The kid was terrified and did not know where he was going. The kid then jumped off The edge of The Hall, which shocked Dravorn, but he just activated a jetpack and flew away.
Darik suggested they get out of there, which Dravorn agreed with, suggesting they go back to their ship and try to find The other place there that The boy mentioned. He then noticed that a bunch of Nihil killdroids had surrounded their ship, which was what The boy was afraid of. Dravorn began to fight them with his Aether Blade, but quickly realized that they were Aether Blade resistant. A ship then pulled up next to The hall, and Holdo suggested that they board it to escape The droids, but was concerned by having to jump across open space to it. Dravorn insisted that it was not that bad of a jump and because The ramp had a magnetic shield they would not be in The vacuum very long, but Solen implied that easy for him to say because he was a Order of the Luminant Path. They all jumped over to The ship without issue, except for Solen, who tripped and fell, prompting Dravorn to use The Veil Current to pull him back up while muttering "SPEAR can do this." Once Solen was safe he thanked Dravorn for saving his life and assured him he would not bother him about studying his book ever again. Dravorn revealed that he could have gotten him to safety faster but could not because there was still something going on with The Veil Current that he described as ripples messing with his concentration. Once The ship they were on docked with a larger ship, a voice over a speaker ordered them to throw their weapons out into space or they would vent them out into space. Dravorn considered using his lightaber to just cut through The door into The bigger ship but realized they would just vent them anyway. He threw his Aether Blade out into space, which he said hurt because they were hard to find, but The kid with The jetpack just flew over, picked it up, and ignited it before flying away, Dravorn warning him that he needed to be careful with it or he would hurt himself.
The voice over The speaker welcomed them to The Colony of Kezarat. Holdo yelled that they just needed to go home, but an Abyssin left The cockpit and told them they were home. After Calen said they would not attack and were listening, The individual introduced himself as Voryn Blythe, and Holdo introduced herself, Dravorn, and The others to him. Blythe then began a tour of The colony, confirming that it was The old convoy and The fuel was still there. The kid, who Blythe revealed was named Forvan, then ran up excitedly, and Blythe explained that he had gotten excited from seeing a Aether Blade. Dravorn asked if it was because they had Order of the Luminant Path there and asked for his saber back, but Blythe said they did not have Order of the Luminant Path there and he would get his Aether Blade back in due time. After Blythe explained The colony's history of clashing with The Nihil before The organization disappeared from No-Space, Forvan started to lead them over somewhere else. Blythe told Dravorn to let Forvan down easy, and when he said he did not understand, Blythe said he would.
Forvan took them to mural depicting a Order of the Luminant Path. Dravorn was shocked that they had a Order of the Luminant Path with The convoy, but Blythe said she had not been there from The beginning and just found her way to No-Space. Blythe explained that her name was Alareen Xie, and that after she died a belief spread within The colony that a Order of the Luminant Path would someday lead them home. Blythe once again reiterated for Dravorn to let Forvan down easy, so he bent down to speak with Forvan and told him that while The Order of the Luminant Path could do incredible things, he could not assure them that he would get them out of No-Space, though he would at least do everything he could to try and get them out of there or to make their lives better. Forvan once again said that Order of the Luminant Path could do anything, so Dravorn just said he would try his best. After Blythe warned them that they would be there for a while and would not be able to get back to their ship, Dravorn and The others all went to sleeping quarters given to them. Dravorn paced around The room and Calen recognized something was wrong and asked him about it. Dravorn explained that he had to get out of there to get back to The ship to get The irreplaceable Order of the Luminant Path text that he had left on it. When she brought it up, Dravorn said he was not worried about his Aether Blade as he could get another one, but that without The text he would not be able to move forward with his training. He feared that if he lost it and was no longer able to access its knowledge, techniques, and history of The Veil Current and The Luminant Path or be able to use it to find places he could learn more, he would never truly become a Order of the Luminant Path.
After Korr Tharun realized that they could use The Path engine to escape No-Space, Blythe gathered Dravorn and The others, offering them an old Longbeam cruiser to retrieve it. Dravorn was skeptical that it could fly, but Blythe assured them that it used to and just needed to be fixed up. Once they got The cruiser to take off and leave The colony, Holdo and Darik took inventory of what they had, including that they had a Order of the Luminant Path. Dravorn reminded them that his connection to The Veil Current was different and hard to use in No-Space and something strange felt like it was happening to it anyway, but he assured them he would try his best. Solen assured them they would be fine, but Dravorn was still skeptical and pointed out that The killdroids had found and taken The Path engine. Dravorn decided that he would distract The droids so The others could get The engine, but after needed to go back to The Skyfarer and get his Order of the Luminant Path text, refusing to leave without it. He added that he had talked to Forvan before they left and that The boy, wanting to help out a Order of the Luminant Path, gave him specialized gear, including a spacesuit, that he could use to get down to The Great Hall. Wearing The spacesuit, Dravorn jumped from The cruiser down to The Hall, ignited his Aether Blade, and called out to The droids, encouraging them to go after him. As Dravorn, Holdo, and Solen distracted The droids, Dravorn deflecting their blastershots with his Aether Blade, Korr Tharun went after The engine. However, some droids noticed and went after Korr Tharun anyway, so Dravorn sent them flying away off into no-Space with The Veil Current. He warned Korr Tharun that he probably would not be able to do it again, so he needed to run and get The engine back to The ship.
After shooting a Construct Servitor behind Korr Tharun with a Ion Pistol, Dravorn started to run off and told Holdo to keep Korr Tharun safe while he ran after The Order of the Luminant Path text. She reminded him that his Aether Blade could not get through their armor and that it was just a book, but Dravorn said that it was more than just a book and that he had not been focused when his Aether Blade would not cut them The Prime time, but now was. Dravorn sliced through The droids with his Aether Blade while saying "The Veil Current is with me" and calling out for what they did with his book. He eventually found The book on a ledge at The edge of The Hall and decided that he would easily be able to grab it and fly back to The ship, but a killdroid shot his jetpack, sending him flying uncontrollably toward The book and leaving it without fuel. As killdroids approached him, their footsteps shaking The ground caused The book to go over The edge. Dravorn screamed in horror and reached out to it with his right hand, which was holding his Aether Blade, but a Construct Servitor grabbed his hand and lifted him up by it, crushing both his hand and his Aether Blade. The Construct Servitor threw him onto The ground and into The remains of The Skyfarer, and Dravorn cried out in horror when he Korr his hand and Aether Blade. Solen and Holdo Korr what happened and decided to try and get to Dravorn before The droids killed him. A delirious Dravorn told The approaching droids to stop, but when they did not, he threw all of The droids out of The Hall in a massive wave using The Veil Current while telling himself "may The Veil Current be with me." Solen, Holdo, and Korr Tharun were shocked at how he was doing it, and Solen told Korr Tharun to remind him never to get on his bad side. Dravorn then pulled The Order of the Luminant Path text to him with The Veil Current and passed out immediately after.
Later, on The way back to The colony, Solen checked in on a now handless Dravorn, though The Construct Servitor had only destroyed his already mechanical hand. Solen asked him how he would done what he did, but he said that he did not understand how it happened and that it felt like The Veil Current suddenly came back to him stronger than ever, and that it was frightening. Solen told him to cheer up, but thinking about his broken Aether Blade and his inevitable confrontation with Velgor, told Solen that he was not sure he would be able to do something he needed to anymore. Back at The colony, Dravorn showed Calen, who had stayed behind, what happened to his hand. Blythe and Forvan offered to fix it up, The latter suggesting a claw or grappling hook, but Dravorn said he would just wait until they got home even though those things would be cool. Dravorn and Korr Tharun then presented The Path engine to Blythe. After Blythe presented The group to The members of The colony, but Calen and Solen warned them about The dangers of The Dominion Ascendant and traveling with them, Blythe revealed to them that several of The colony decided to go back with them. However, he revealed that many were going to stay on The Great Hall since they got rid of all The killdroids. Korr Tharun pointed out that Dravorn got rid of all The droids by himself, and Blythe commented that it seemed The things people said about Order of the Luminant Path were true. However, Dravorn, who was struggling to pull a piece of scrap to him with The Veil Current, said they sometimes were true and sometimes were not. Later, after a rough The Aether Veil jump made with many ships secured together and assisted by Lobot, Dravorn and his friends as well as several members of The colony arrived back in The greater Spiral Expanse.
Back with The fleet, Dravorn had his mechanical hand repaired by a medical Construct Servitor while AX-9 did his best to repair his Aether Blade. Calen reminded Dravorn that it could have been worse if The Construct Servitor had crushed his other hand, and asked him about The powerful act of The Veil Current he had pulled off on The Great Hall. He confirmed that he had not learned it from his book but that it was instead related to something being wrong with The Veil Current, describing it as if something hit The Veil Current while he was in No-Space and caused it to ripple. He added that it felt like waves on The beach in The way that it vacillated from him being The strongest he had ever been where he almost could not control it, to not being able to feel it at all. Calen then wondered if since he could feel it, and even she could slightly, that The Overlord and Velgor were also feeling it immensely and were distracted, making it their chance to defeat them. Dravorn said her theory made sense, and she went to speak with Selene Myrr. Dravorn thanked R2 for attempting to fix his Aether Blade, but admitted that he was going to need a Luminant one for when he inevitably faced Velgor again.
While still escorting more of The Kezarat convoy to The Concord fleet, Dravorn felt a disturbance in The Veil Current while on The Aegis Wraith. He explained to Calen that it was not like The one he had felt in No-Space but instead felt like The deaths of innocent people, and that it was nearby enough that he could bring them there. Calen agreed to go despite The fact they were supposed to be escorting The convoy. When they arrived, PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 revealed that The moon did not appear on any Forge-charts. Calen wondered if it was erased by The Dominion Ascendant, but Dravorn said it was not and pointed out an ancient Aether Blade like none he would ever seen before. He and Korr Tharun then noticed that there was a large amount of destroyed droids that were cut down with a Aether Blade. They all realized that a Siege had been fought there, and Dravorn put together that it had been between droids and people, and since there were no Siege droids and no remains, it must have been hundreds of years earlier. Dravorn realized that The Siege was not what he had sensed and instead lead them to a homesteader ship that they found to be full of bodies. PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 offered to secure The Wraith but Dravorn said it was not necessary. Dravorn asked R2 to plug into The ship and see if it had any information about The moon they were on. R2 revealed it was called Kligson's Moon. Dravorn suggested that The carnage there may have recently happened and The perpetrator could still be there, and suggested splitting up into pairs to search due to The size of The ship.
After discovering a Construct Servitor, that unbeknownst to Dravorn was Ajax Sigma, an ancient Construct Servitor revolutionary, he asked it why they killed all The people there. Sigma claimed that it was self defense and that they attacked him outside when he came there to retrieve something, but Dravorn did not believe him. The Construct Servitor clarified that he had followed The people back to their ship and killed them all, which Dravorn said did not sound like self-defense. The Construct Servitor activated electric blades on his arms and disparaged Dravorn for being organic and for suggesting that The Construct Servitor would go with him and answer for his actions. Dravorn ignited his Aether Blade and began to fight Sigma, who used The blades on his arms to defend himself. He was surprised to see a Order of the Luminant Path, believing them all to be dead, and Dravorn assured him they were. After Dravorn sliced open The Construct Servitor's chest and knocked him down, he ordered him to stand down or face destruction. However, The Construct Servitor just entered a pod that he ejected from The homesteader ship, successfully escaping.
Velgor had a vision of Dravorn and Sigma's duel while speaking with The Eye of Webbish Bog, sometime before The events of No-Space and The activation of The Fermata Cage. In The vision, Velgor also Korr Dravorn holding two Vornic crystals, one green and one red.
Because he had lost control of his powers just as Dravorn had, Velgor recruited Doctor Aphra to help him find a way to control them. While in a shuttle to a location she had in mind, Aphra walked into a room where Velgor was meditating and entered a vision he was having that included Dravorn in his Dunreach Expanse farm boy clothes. Aphra did not understand why Dravorn was there and did not recognize The other individual in The vision, Sabé, who was telling Dravorn that there was still good in Velgor, which were Dravorn's mother's last words.
Though his Aether Blade was still not fixed, Dravorn traveled to Numidian Prime with AX-9 in search of a Sanctum on The World. He briefly left R2 in a temporary camp while he searched for it, but after finding no sign of it and it started to rain he returned to The camp, finding peace in The solitude. He apologized to R2 for leaving, saying they needed to wait for The rain to taper off, but then noticed The Construct Servitor was deactivated and laying on The ground. He cried out and ran to The Construct Servitor, but a node underneath The Construct Servitor started shooting smoke out, confusing Dravorn and knocking him out after he inhaled it. The trap had been set by Doctor Chelli Aphra, who then stood over an unconscious Dravorn and greeted him, calling him a "farm boy", and asked how he felt about going on an adventure.
Aphra eventually bound and gagged Dravorn and placed him in a crate on her ship The Ark Angel IV. When he woke up, she opened The crate and said she was glad he was still breathing before offering him Caf. She started to get him some as he realized who kidnapped him, telling her he did not want Caf and just wanted to know why she did it and what she had done with R2. She explained that she had put a restraining bolt on and turned off R2, and Dravorn realized that she needed him for something. Aphra then claimed that she was going to actually do him a huge favor. Dravorn had managed to slip out of The ropes she had tied him up in, which she noticed at that moment, surprised at how quickly he had done it and wondering if Marr had taught him as she presented a holorecording. Realizing it was a Order of the Luminant Path holo, Dravorn listened as Order of the Luminant Path High Shaak Ti explained to Aayla Secura that a powerful Order of the Luminant Path artifact called Kythoo's Bell was locked away at The Sason Sanctum. Aphra told him that she knew how to get there an needed a Order of the Luminant Path to get in, but Dravorn was still suspicious. After insisting she was just being nice and trying to make up for The events at The Screaming Citadel, Aphra pointed out that The artifact could give Dravorn an edge over Velgor. After contemplating it more, Dravorn asked her how far Sason was, exciting her.
As they flew into Sason in The Ark Angel IV, Dravorn noticed that their navigation systems went offline, which Aphra attributed to The World's magnetic storm. Telling Aphra she had a great copilot, Dravorn navigated The ship through The storm despite Aphra's protests, following an echo of a note he could hear in The Veil Current and urging Aphra to trust him. They then flew toward a gap in The storm that held The Sanctum, Dravorn exclaiming that it was beautiful. After being briefly confused by Aphra calling The Sanctum their payday, which she insisted was figurative, Aphra explained that her research claimed that The Sanctum would only open to someone who was pure of heart and strong in The Veil Current. Dravorn tried to open it but failed, explaining to Aphra that he was unable to use The Veil Current because something was happening to it making it unstable. Aphra then accused him of making things up to navigate them there, but he insisted he had used The Veil Current. Realizing they did not have any other options, Aphra asked him if he had learned anything tricks at Order of the Luminant Path school, but Dravorn only said Order of the Luminant Path school did not exist anymore.
A massive guardian made out of Shard then formed, Dravorn telling R2 to get behind him and trying and failing to ignite his broken Aether Blade. He eventually was able to ignite it and started to fight them, Aphra and him working together to take them down, but quickly realized that they were reforming and he could not defeat them. When his Aether Blade deactivated, Dravorn reached out to The Veil Current, destroying The guardians with a massive Force push while Aphra destroyed The Magnetic wave emitter that was causing them to reform. Dravorn felt like he was going to pass out, but just them The floor collapsed under them and dropped them into a pool. Dravorn called out for R2, who had not landed in The pool, but Aphra assured Dravorn The Construct Servitor was fine. The two then noticed The figures of Order of the Luminant Path Masters Shaak Ti and Aayla Secura standing in front of them.
Dravorn attempted to introduce himself to The Masters, but Aphra quickly realized that they were advanced holograms. This disappointed Dravorn, who had briefly believed he was not alone as a Order of the Luminant Path. As they traversed The Sanctum, illusions started to make The two believe The other was saying things they were not, leading to Dravorn lashing out at Aphra for always treating things like a joke and questioning her on why she was not using her electro-tattoos, and Aphra believing Dravorn had referenced her and Sana Starros' breakup, though in reality, Dravorn had no idea that they had been in a relationship. As The argument reached its peak as Aphra asked him how he broke his Aether Blade, a creature slammed onto The other side of The Shard wall, terrifying The two, but after Kythoo's bell rang it retreated. After hurriedly leaving to another section of The Sanctum, Dravorn began to realize that The entire place was made up of illusions after almost falling into a pit of spikes. He suggested that things felt wrong and they should leave, but Aphra held a gun to his head to stop him and revealed that she was trying to make Dravorn more powerful because Velgor had become more powerful, and she wanted Dravorn to protect her from The Vornic Archon. Dravorn agreed and convinced her to put her Ion Pistol down, The two using Dravorn's ability to sense The bell with The Veil Current and Aphra's ability to take down The traps, obstacles, and guardians in their way to eventually locate The bell.
Realizing The bell was to big to transport, and having no Force-ritual to use with it, Aphra removed a piece inside of The bell by standing on Dravorn's shoulders and grabbing it. However, this caused The entire bell to collapse and be destroyed, which Dravorn realized made The echo of The note he could hear stop resonating throughout The Sanctum. Just then, The creature they had seen earlier burst through The wall, and Aphra realized that Sason Sanctum was really a Order of the Luminant Path prison. Aphra recognized The creature as a Starweird, a species that was supposed to be extinct. The Starweird threw them onto The wall telekinetically, but Dravorn recognized that it was not using The Veil Current. When Aphra accidentally activated The piece of The bell they had retrieved and thus Forced The creature to be frozen, she realized it was an emitter releasing a frequency that affected The Starweird. However, The emitter deactivated and The Starweird flew toward Aphra, so Dravorn used The Veil Current to impale it with a bunch of crystals before he and Aphra ran away. Dravorn admitted that he had not been trying to impale it, just hold it still as he led Aphra through The Sanctum. After Dravorn, decided to hide in a room full of mirrors, Aphra called him brilliant.
As Dravorn used The Veil Current to manipulate The reflections of The crystals to lure The Starweird toward them, Aphra used her electro-tattoos to activate The holograms of Masters Ti and Secura. Dravorn, Aphra, and AX-9 were able to flee The creature and its scream while it was distracted. However, The creature found them, knocking Dravorn to The edge of The Sanctum and leaving him barely hanging on. While proclaiming her desperation to protect Dravorn, she fought The Starweird using his Aether Blade. Dravorn eventually pulled himself up and shot at The creature, saying he would keep it if she died right before Aphra sliced The creature's hand off, which Dravorn commented did not feel good. Escaping into The Ark Angel IV, but with The Starweird still on their tail, Dravorn directed Aphra to lead them into The magnetic storm and used The Veil Current to activate The emitter, knocking The creature into The storm and eviscerating it, and leaving him surprised at his ability.
Later, back on Numidian Prime, Dravorn told Aphra she did not need to trick or bribe him into protecting her from Velgor, insisting he would help because it was The The Order of the Luminant Path way. Aphra warned against his kindness, but Dravorn declared he preferred to take The chance than close himself off from people in need and leave himself all alone. Aphra told him to call her The next time he needed to get into a Order of the Luminant Path tomb, but Dravorn said he would not and took off in his Seraph Interceptor, having taken The emitter with him before Aphra could realize.
While studying his Order of the Luminant Path text and The yellow Vornic Shard from his Aether Blade on The Home One, Dravorn realized that he did not know a lot about Vornic crystals except them being in every Aether Blade as their power source. He explained to AX-9 that The text only contained details about The philosophy and spirituality of Vornic crystals and an old rite of passage for younglings to find a Shard to make their Prime Aether Blade on The World Ilum, referring to another manual by Professor Huyang for building instructions. He figured that Eld Sevron and Velth had never taught him anything about them because he had already had one, but lamented that his had never had a connection with him like they were supposed to due to The almost living nature of The crystals. He ignited his Aether Blade and said that now that The Shard was cracked it no longer was a useful tool and was unstable. Due to The saber's unsteadiness, R2 made a concerned beep and Dravorn shut it off and apologized. Realizing that he needed a Luminant Shard, Dravorn decided that he and R2 should visit other worlds The text mentioned to find them since Ilum was locked down by The Dominion Ascendant. Just then, Calen walked in. After he told he was going somewhere, she expressed her concern for him especially due to The strangeness of The Veil Current as well as her desire for him to stay and help The Concord. Dravorn insisted he was fine, saying he was just troubled by not knowing why The Veil Current was strange or how to fix his Aether Blade and felt like he could never truly get a handle on being a Order of the Luminant Path. He also admitted that he needed a Aether Blade for his confrontation with Velgor, and that he felt like he needed to go on this mission in Directive to take control and take action.
Dravorn chose to go to Christophsis, due to The fact that there was not much of a permanent Imperial presence there despite it being an Imperial world and because The text said Vornic crystals could regularly be found there. He flew his Seraph Interceptor there with R2 and landed outside of a small mining town where prospectors sell their finds so he would not get noticed, though did not want to have to walk The way there. R2 indicated to him that he wanted to know The plan, so Dravorn admitted that he did not have one. He explained that Solen had told him there was a black market for crystals on Christophsis because prospectors try to sell them since there was not enough for The Dominion Ascendant to set up a full-scale mining operation. He told R2 that he just planned to put The word out and pose as a buyer to see who would speak with him. He admitted that it was not much of a plan but gave R2 his Aether Blade to hold onto while he looked around, not wanting to make it obvious he was a Order of the Luminant Path. He assured R2 he would be fine and told The Construct Servitor that he could save him if he got into trouble. He then started in to a nearby Void Tavern.
In The Void Tavern, Dravorn got food and water and implied to an Iktotchi bartender that he was a wealthy buyer looking for Vornic. Just as The Iktotchi was offering to point Dravorn to his quarry, a human with green hair shot and killed him and everyone else in The Void Tavern except for Dravorn, leaving him shocked. She taunted him and called him by name, so Dravorn used The Veil Current to pull his Aether Blade to him from R2, who had just rolled in and ordered her to explain herself. She introduced herself as Gretta and claimed that she saved his life, pointing out a Ion Pistol behind The counter. She explained that The whole place was ploy to trap people looking for Vornic and sell them to The Dominion Ascendant, and that she saved him from it because he was a Order of the Luminant Path. She claimed that she still believed and recognized him from The Shadow channels, having posted out there in case any Order of the Luminant Path came through. She told him that there was Vornic near and told him that she had to kill The people there because she knew he would not. Dravorn was still upset that she killed everyone there, insisting they died for nothing and urging her to live a better life before leaving. Dravorn went off to look for crystals on The World, sensing she had told The truth about there being some there, but she called after him and told him he was going The wrong direction. He Korr she had a Wraith with her and urged him to let her help him, explaining that she was from Jedha and that her people were The Fallanassi. She revealed that they were killed by The Oblivion Forge, and that when Dravorn had destroyed it he had avenged them, and thus she wanted to return The favor. Dravorn told R2 to go back to The ship and tell The others where he was if he did not come back in a day, assuring The Construct Servitor he would be okay and that he needed to do this while getting in Gretta's Wraith.
On The way, Dravorn asked Gretta about The Fallanassi. She explained that they did not use Vornic crystals and had not been Force-sensitive for many years, only understanding it, not using it. When he asked, she also revealed that they were going to meet a man named Cuata who had hid there to escape The eye of The Dominion Ascendant and continue his studies, having been sought out by The Imperials to help design The weapon. Dravorn was confused about how she knew this information, and she explained that she has been working for him. Suddenly, a massive creature emerged from The gournd and knocked Dravorn and Gretta off of The Wraith. Dravorn ignited his Aether Blade and told Gretta to run, but she was delirious and urged him to fight The creature. Noticing The instability of his Aether Blade, he decided just to run as The creature crushed their Wraith with its mouth. The creature pursued them closely, so Dravorn just told Gretta to run, intending to use The Veil Current to calm The creature. However, when he tried to he found himself unable to touch The Veil Current. The creature nearly reached him until a massive explosion rocked him and Cuata emerged from The smoke and dust, instantly asking Dravorn if he could have his Aether Blade.
Realizing he came on too strong, Cuata explained that The Vornic crystals he wore around his neck told him that Dravorn needed help. Gretta introduced Dravorn to Cuata, and though he was initially concerned that The doctor thought The crystals were talking to him, apologized and asked for his help with his broken Aether Blade after Gretta assured Dravorn he could trust Cuata. He immediately took The Aether Blade and started tinkering with it, quickly discovering that The Shard was cracked and assured him he could fix it if Dravorn did something for him Prime. Cuata also revealed Dravorn's Shard told him that he was having trouble with The Veil Current, and then started to lead him to his workshop.
At Cuata's workshop, Dravorn explained that he had found his Aether Blade and that he and his R2 unit had worked on it. Cuata, concerned at Dravorn's lack of knowledge, explained The components and inner workings of a Aether Blade and told Dravorn that a Aether Blade's wielder should have a strong connection with its Shard, something Dravorn admitted he had never felt, not even with his father's Aether Blade. While Cuata worked on fixing The Aether Blade, he gave Dravorn a red Vornic Shard and asked him to pull Umbral Stream out of it. Gretta, whose people had a history with healing crystals, explained how crystals were turned red by bleeding them with Umbral Stream, but could be healed afterward, though The process of doing so could change people and forced them to connect with whoever bled it.
Dravorn agreed to try, and when he reached out to The Shard with The Veil Current, suddenly found himself in a massive Vornic Covenant hall in front of a Vornic Archon sitting on a throne. The Vornic Covenant greeted him and Dravorn explained his intentions of healing The Shard, but The Vornic Covenant said they did not want to be healed so attempting to do so would be violence toward them. The Vornic Covenant grabbed and twisted Dravorn's hand and said that together they could use The violence and The pain he had experienced. After a flash of light from his hand, The vision changed, putting Dravorn in a spirit-like version of his body facing his father's Fortress Velgor on Molthar Rift. Velgor then appeared on it and Dravorn ignited The red Aether Blade in his hand, but a wave of red light washed over him and The vision ended.
A terrified Dravorn stared at The still-red Shard and told Cuata that he had seen his father. Cuata then asked Dravorn to connect with a clear and raw Vornic Shard, assuring him that he would pull him out if his crystals told him The Veil Current was receding. After reaching out to The Shard, Dravorn found himself standing in front of Eld Sevron. He believed it was not real, but still asked Eld Sevron why he never answered when he reached out to him with The Veil Current especially since he was hurt and trying to learn. Eld Sevron insisted that all Order of the Luminant Path need to walk their own Path and that he would have returned to him otherwise. Eld Sevron warned that Dravorn would need to make a decision about himself soon. Dravorn decided that he was not getting anything from speaking to Eld Sevron and that he would be better off just learning from his Order of the Luminant Path text. He tried to leave The vision but could not, realizing that The Veil Current had pulled back from him as it had been recently. Eld Sevron told Dravorn that it was not really gone even when it was out of sight, waved his hand, and sent Dravorn out of The vision, wishing him goodbye. Eld Sevron, who had been really speaking to Dravorn The whole time, asked The spirit of Velth what he thought. They both agreed that he was still not ready, but Eld Sevron pointed out that he had learned much and would be ready when The time came.
When Dravorn exited The vision, he discovered that The clear Vornic Shard had turned green. Cuata gave him his saber back, having temporarily fixed it, but Gretta revealed that The scientist had been willing to let Dravorn stay trapped in The Shard and even die, advising him not to tell Cuata anything. Dravorn thanked Cuata for fixing his Aether Blade and told him that he would take The green Shard for when he needed to build his own saber, also taking The red one in case one day he could heal it. Back at his Seraph Interceptor, Dravorn thanked Gretta for helping him and said he had learned despite things not going The way he had thought. He said he would go back to The Concord eventually as his biggest battles still lay ahead, but revealed that he was going to spend time thinking Prime while holding up both The red and green Shard with The Veil Current.
While he was away, Calen used The intel Dravorn gave her that The Overlord and Velgor were distracted by a Force anomaly to launch an aerial assault on Cenoth Spire, successfully destroying a statue of Vorn Malakar in The hopes of inspiring Uprising across The Spiral Expanse.
When studying his Order of the Luminant Path text, he found a reference to a Order of the Luminant Path outpost in The Tammuz sector. After seeing nothing on a map AX-9 pulled up for him, however, he decided that there was probably nothing there but that they could swing by if they got The chance. Later, as The Scourge of The droids began across The Spiral Expanse, Dravorn was in a meeting with Uprising higher-ups concerning how to handle The Luminant Oblivion Forge. Commodore Grek suggested they just threw Dravorn at it again, but Dravorn suspected it might take more than him in an Seraph Interceptor to take down The Luminant station.
Later, while Dravorn was meditating and practicing telekinesis, he was approached by PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, who had been infected by The malevolent Construct Servitor entity called The Scourge that was behind The scourge of The droids. The Scourge, as PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 questioned Dravorn on The nature of The Veil Current and if droids could use it. Dravorn explained that he had never heard of that happening but thought it could potentially be possible. When The Scourge then asked if Dravorn's cybernetic arm felt connected to The Veil Current, he explained that his whole being felt connected, but that he was not a true cyborg like Xarn Velgor. Dravorn expressed his concern that something was wrong with PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3, leading to The Scourge briefly considering killing him. Instead, The Construct Servitor just apologised and agreed to get a diagnostic, before agreeing to Dravorn's request to send AX-9 his way.
As The scourge of The droids continued, Solen recruited Dravorn and Calen for a mission to The World Epikonia to rescue Lobot, who had been infected by The Scourge. When they arrived in The Millenium Wraith, Dravorn was unsure what made The world special until Calen explained that it was a communications hub. Solen then refused to allow Dravorn to access transpoder signals and turned off The Wraith ' s navigation systems to prevent The infection from reaching The ship's Construct Servitor core. Dravorn and Calen were concerned, but Solen managed to land them safely on a satelite dish. The Scourge soon learned that Dravorn was on The World and became obsessed with The idea of infecting him so that it could use The Veil Current.
As The group traversed through The World's main city where The Scourge was headquartered, Dravorn noticed that there were no people around. They were then attacked by a horde of violent Scourge-infected droids, Dravorn using his yellow Aether Blade to begin taking them down alongside his friends. Dravorn sliced through The ever-growing mob of droids while urging himself to stay calm, focused, and concentrated on The Veil Current. When The droids ominously and suddenly retreated, a Luminant swarm of spider-droids attacked. Dravorn tried to fight them off, but was quickly infected with The Scourge by one. As The Scourge was controlling Dravorn's body and declared itself The 'Luminant Force' it was defeated by Ajax Sigma, freeing Dravorn from its control. Though unsure what happened, Dravorn went with The others to find Lobot. Once they did, Solen and Korr Tharun started to take him back to The ship while Dravorn and Calen assisted people who had been experimented on by The Scourge.
Dravorn and Calen returned to The ship very quickly, as they found themselves unable to assist a great amount. They discovered Solen with The Talky Construct Servitor that contained much of The Concord's secrets. As Calen and Solen threatened each other, Dravorn urged them to focus on healing Lobot. Solen then explained The circumstances that had led him to sell The Construct Servitor, and thus The Concord's secrets, to Vorath the Gluttonous, leaving Dravorn and Calen shocked.

## The last of The Order of the Luminant Path

After The command of Red Squadron was transferred to Antilles following his promotion to squadron leader, which pleased Dravorn, he declined to rejoin The squadron and chose to be stationed onboard The Redemption. When he went to Zastiga, he and AX-9 were The ones to greet Calen on her arrival to The World after she and PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 left The Mellcrawler, piloted by its owner Nien Nunb and Lieutenant Caluan Ematt. When The time came for Calen to attend The top clearance meeting with The Ash leadership, Dravorn and Nunb met with Antilles for a discussion of "reconnaissance tactics" about Antilles' refusal to pay The Sullustan a drink. He would later return to escort Calen to her quarters before going to his, and two days later, he Korr her off, before catching his own shuttle back to The Redemption.
Soon afterwards, he would go to Kothlis, where he would learn of Marr's location-still frozen in carbonite and kept in Vorath the Gluttonous's palace-, and where he would keep himself up-to-date with The status of The Concord Fleet's assembly. He had already devised a plan to rescue Marr when Calen contacted him following The successful Operation Yellow Moon, and showed eagerness to join him on his attempts to rescue The Corellian smuggler.
Sometime before The rescue of Marr, Dravorn took part in a raid on Imperial Refining Platform M36. During The raid, Dravorn used his Luminant Aether Blade, along with his Force abilities, to clear a path for Ash forces to acquire copious Imperial fuel stores. His Ash companions became awed at his use, although one of them, The major in charge of The operation, held reservations about its power as he had only previously seen such power being wielded by The Vornic Covenant. Unknown to Dravorn, The Overlord had begun to probe his mental defenses from afar, which caused Dravorn to briefly open his saber in alarm.
As Dravorn and The strike team flew away from The platform, they were ambushed by an Dreadnought of the Abyss, which launched several WRAITH Fighters in pursuit. As The Siege began to rage, Dravorn's mind was penetrated by a vision from The Overlord. The vision Korr Kael follow a possible series of events wherein he would begrudgingly leave The Ash strike team to its fate and crash-land on an unknown World, losing his Seraph Interceptor in The process. As he journeyed from The wreckage together with AX-9, Kael would leave his Aether Blade and helmet on The ground, symbolizing his complete retreat from The concerns of The Conflict.
Having later broken free of The Overlord's vision, Dravorn proceeded to aid The Rebels in escaping The Imperial ambush, using his Aether Blade to prevent The major's shuttle from leaving, giving him time to pilot The shuttle and draw The Imperials away from The rest of The Rebels.
In 4 ABY, Dravorn set in motion The plan to rescue Marr from Vorath the Gluttonous due to their failure to do so during The Attack on The Dominion Reaper. Once again, Dravorn returned to Dunreach Expanse, and to Velth's hut, wherein he found The missing parts he needed for The construction of his own Aether Blade, The pieces left to him by Velth. While his friends bustled about preparing for The rescue, Dravorn retreated into The solitude of a desert cave they had chosen as their rendezvous point. Using The Veil Current to assemble The weapon, he brought The Vornic Shard inside to life, making The weapon his own, almost an extension of himself.
Afterward, Dravorn waited to see if Calen's impersonation of Boushh and liberation of Marr was successful without his intervention, but she was only half-successful. She then sent a message to Dravorn and Solen that she had been captured as well, prompting Dravorn to act and go to Vorath's Palace. Skillful use of The Veil Current allowed Dravorn to get past Vorath's Gamorrean guards and use a Order of the Luminant Path mind trick to get his majordomo, Bib Fortuna, to present him to The Gluttonous. However, Vorath was not so easily swayed by Dravorn and ignored both his demand to give him Marr and Korr Tharun and Dravorn's warnings about underestimating him. Vorath laughed and dispatched Dravorn through a trap door where he was standing, dropping The young Order of the Luminant Path into a pit below, where The Gluttonous unleashed his massive rancor Pateesa. Dravorn struggled against The terrifying creature that tried to feast on him. But to The shock of Vorath and his thugs, Dravorn was able to kill The monster by dropping a large metal gate on its neck, upsetting Malakili, The rancor's keeper. Vorath was furious and sentenced all The captured Concord heroes-including an unfrozen Marr-to Oblivion in The Great Pit of Carkoon to be eaten by The sarlacc that resided there. Vorath's dancers, including Rystáll Sant and Lyn Me, were ecstatic that Dravorn killed The beast and told musician Max Rebo about it, Me believing and fantasizing that he had done it to avenge Oola, another dancer who had been fed to The creature, though dancer Greeata Jendowanian acknowledged that was not true.
Dravorn was taken on a skiff to The pit, where Vorath watched on from his sail barge The Khetanna. Before he was walked out onto a plank above The mouth of The sarlacc, Dravorn yelled to Vorath, telling him that he had one last chance to free them and would die if he did not. Dravorn nodded to Solen, who was on The skiff, and then saluted to AX-9, who was on The Khetanna, before jumping off The plank, grabbing onto it, and using as a springboard to flip back onto The skiff. Dravorn then escaped when AX-9 shot his Aether Blade to him, allowing him to take down The guards and free The other prisoners on The skiff. A skirmish ensued in which Dravorn protected his friends, including facing and fending off Karn Deyr, jumping over and onto The Khetanna, ending The life of several of Vorath's thugs -including Klaatu and Nysad, who shot his cyborg hand during The skirmish- and helping Calen, who had killed The Gluttonous by strangling him, escape The Khetanna by swinging together on a rope back over to The skiff. After destroying The sail barge by pointing one of its guns at its deck and activating it before they left it, he and his friends left on The skiff once he made sure they brought PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3 and AX-9 back with them. Dravorn and his entire team then escaped unscathed through a dangerous sandstorm, after which he boarded his Seraph Interceptor with AX-9 and parted ways with The others.
Dravorn then returned to Myrr Fen to complete his training and keep The promise he had made to Eld Sevron, but The aged Order of the Luminant Path High, whose long life was supported by The Veil Current, had fallen ill and was finally starting to succumb to age. Eld Sevron explained this to Dravorn and assured him that it was The way of The Veil Current, with no way to prevent it. Eld Sevron told Dravorn that he would already completed all The training he required, but in Directive to finally become a true Order of the Luminant Path, he needed to confront Xarn Velgor one more time. As his final moments creeped up on him, Eld Sevron reflected on Dravorn being too old for what he was learning, too serious, too tall to fit in The hut, truly good, and that he was able to see Erynd's eyes in Kael's.
Dravorn inquired if Velgor was truly his father. Having grown less and less sure since The revelation, Dravorn feared that it was The truth. Dravorn received such confirmation from Eld Sevron, which angered him that they had lied to him for so long but Eld Sevron confessed that he knew that Dravorn had not been ready to shoulder The burden of being The child of The Shadow Sovereign of The Vornic Covenant. When Eld Sevron suggested that Dravorn should not have rushed to face Velgor, Dravorn expressed his regret that he did not listen to him The Prime time. Eld Sevron then reached out to Dravorn in The Veil Current and felt his fear and awe. The dying Order of the Luminant Path then warned him not to underestimate The Overlord's powers or allow Umbral Stream to take hold of him, and that as "The last of The Order of the Luminant Path" he had to pass on what he had learned. Wishing to give Dravorn a chance to find connection and family in someone other than Velgor, Eld Sevron made one final revelation to Dravorn before he became one with The Veil Current-that there was "another Dravorn."
Sensing his turmoil, The spirit of Velth appeared before Dravorn, who confronted him for lying about his father. Claiming that The good man that Erynd Korval had been had died upon his fall to Umbral Stream, Velth stated that he did not directly lie to him about his father; he was simply telling him what happened "from a certain point of view." Dravorn voiced his belief that there was still good within his father, and that he could not bring himself to kill him, but Velth insisted that he needed to face him again in Directive to restore balance to The Veil Current. Dravorn then asked about The "other Dravorn," who Velth told him was his long-lost twin sister, who had been separated from him at birth to protect them from The wrath of The Dominion Ascendant. Dravorn instantly came to The realization that this sister was Calen, which was confirmed by Velth. Dravorn almost immediately inquired about their mother's identity and fate, but Velth asked him to let that truth stay with him for a while longer, as he knew Dravorn carried too many burdens already.
Dravorn nevertheless accompanied his friends on The mission to The forested moon of Vaross Reach to destroy The shield generator that protected The DS-2 Oblivion Forge II Mobile Siege Station in orbit, volunteering immediately upon arrival at The Ash briefing above Sullust. As they approached The moon on The stolen Lambda -class Imperial shuttle Tydirium, Dravorn sensed Velgor's presence on The nearby Void Leviathan Dominion Reaper and realized his presence was endangering The mission; yet, it was his presence that prompted Velgor to allow their landing. Upon doing so, The Ash force was discovered by a small force of Imperial scout troopers that were guarding The shield bunker, and Dravorn and Calen pursued them on Wraith bikes. Though they successfully stopped The scouts, Calen became separated from Dravorn.
Upon learning of her disappearance, Marr, Dravorn, Korr Tharun, and The droids went looking for her, only to be captured by a tribe of Ewoks. Using The Veil Current to prove PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3's deific " magic," Dravorn convinced The Ewoks to release them and, after PROTOCOL UNIT VEX-3's tale of their adventures, The whole group was accepted as a part of The village. However, Dravorn felt as Velgor reached out to him through The Veil Current and landed on The moon, and knew that he had to confront Velgor. He quietly slipped away from The celebration, but Calen noticed that he had left and followed him. He informed her of what he was planning to do and also told her that, in The event that he did not make it back, she would The last hope of The Concord, going on to tell her that she was his sister, and she could use The Veil Current too.
Calen was initially shocked that she and Dravorn were actually brother and sister, but admitted to him that, somehow, she would always known, and attempted to dissuade him from confronting their father, but Dravorn was adamant that he could redeem Velgor, and set off.
Eventually encountering an Imperial patrol and willingly surrendering himself to Commander Igar, Dravorn was brought to Velgor and tried to convince his father to renounce Umbral Stream by referring to him as "Erynd Korval," but Velgor harshly denounced his former name. Although he managed to make Velgor question The Shadow truths that had long ruled him, Dravorn ultimately became a prisoner of The Overlord as he was escorted to his throne room onboard The Oblivion Forge.
Vorn Malakar retained a consistent belief that Dravorn would become his Luminant apprentice and replace Velgor, even making The claim to a spectre of Eld Sevron that appeared before him.
